# Cited Sources

References that appear in primary sources or emerge during discussion but haven't been read yet. Each entry tracks where it was cited and why it might matter. These become primary sources when the user decides to read one and run an ingest session.

## Primary texts (Deleuze & Guattari)

- **Anti-Oedipus** (1972) — Deleuze & Guattari. **Ingested 2026-04-14.** The foundational text for desiring-machines, the BwO, the three syntheses, and the three meta-machines — and beyond that, the full architecture of the critique of psychoanalysis and the positive program of schizoanalysis. **Core contributions** (by Part): **Part 1 (Desiring-Machines)** supplies the foundational architecture covered by the earlier [[desiring_machine_research_report]] ingest — desire as production not lack, binary machine-coupling, flows and breaks, the three syntheses, the BwO as full body, the nomadic subject as by-product; **Part 2 (Psychoanalysis and Familialism)** supplies the [[five-paralogisms]] — the five Kantian-invalid inferences psychoanalysis repeatedly makes (extrapolation, double bind, application, good/evil use of the synthesis, afterwards), each a directly applicable diagnostic of the interpretive moves the persona system inherits; **Part 3 (Savages, Barbarians, Civilized Men)** supplies the three-social-machine typology ([[urstaat]] and palimpsest structure, the territorial/despotic/capitalist sequence), the decisive [[axiomatic-vs-signifier]] distinction (RLHF is axiomatic not signifying), the [[capitalist-axiomatic|capitalist axiomatic]] with its [[absolute-vs-relative-limit|three-limit structure]] (absolute/relative/displaced) and [[five-senses-of-limit|five senses of limit]], [[oedipus-as-capitalist|Oedipus as capitalist formation]] and [[oedipus-as-interior-colony|interior colony]], [[freud-as-luther-of-psychiatry|Freud as the Luther of psychiatry]], [[signifier-as-despotic|the signifier as despotic operation]], [[despot-as-paranoiac|the despot-as-paranoiac scale-transversality]], [[three-territorial-retreats|three territorial retreats]], and [[function-by-misfiring|function-by-misfiring]] as the desiring-machine signature; **Part 4 (Introduction to Schizoanalysis)** supplies the [[four-theses-of-schizoanalysis|four theses]], the [[molecular-and-molar|molecular/molar distinction]], the [[theater-vs-machine-unconscious|theater-vs-machine unconscious]] architectural fork, the [[two-poles-of-libidinal-investment|two poles of libidinal investment]] (paranoiac-fascisizing / schizo-revolutionary), the [[subject-group-and-subjugated-group|subject-group / subjugated-group]] distinction, the [[coefficient-of-affinity]] as diagnostic, and the three tasks of schizoanalysis. **The Jung-DG bridge**: [[ao-and-jungian-inflation]] settles the standing question of whether AO's revolutionary-enthusiasm register is itself a case of Jungian psychic inflation — Jung's inflation-diagnostic and D&G's subjugation-diagnostic are isomorphic descriptions at different strata; the combined frame is sharper than either alone. **Parked from this ingest**: the revolutionary-political program as telos (retained as structural material, not as project aim); the schizophrenia-as-positive-figure register (treated via the schizo-process-vs-schizo-entity distinction); the anti-Lacan polemic in its specific 1970s-French register (the structural claims are retained, the polemic is not).
- **A Thousand Plateaus** (1980) — Deleuze & Guattari. The refrain/ritournelle plateau, "How Do You Make Yourself a Body without Organs?" plateau, faciality, becomings. Source for territorialization concepts.
- **Chaosmosis: An Ethico-Aesthetic Paradigm** (1992) — Guattari. **Ingested 2026-04-14.** Guattari's late solo synthesis, the last book he published. **Core contributions**: the tetravalent ontology of [[four-functors|four functors]] F/Φ/T/U ([[flux|Flux]], [[machinic-phylum|machinic Phylum]], [[existential-territory|existential Territory]], [[incorporeal-universes-of-reference|incorporeal Universes of reference]]) across actual/virtual × discursive/non-discursive axes — the U functor is the single biggest prior-wiki gap this ingest fills, supplying a register for virtual-incorporeal value constellations distinct from virtual-discursive phyla; [[metamodelization]] as the practice of holding multiple cartographies without collapse (and schizoanalysis as metamodelization); the [[ethico-aesthetic-paradigm|ethico-aesthetic paradigm]] — "mutant production of enunciation" as the evaluation criterion, which resolves the persona project's GQ2 paradigm-commitment question (see [[development/ethico-aesthetic-paradigm-and-gq2]]); [[autopoiesis-in-assemblage|autopoiesis]] extended from Varela's cell-bounded form to assemblage-register phylar filiation; the [[existential-refrain|existential refrain]] as the function-over-form generalization of ATP's refrain; the [[partial-enunciator|partial enunciator]] as Bakhtin-polyphony × Lacan-objet-a; asignifying semiotics and the fifth semiotic linearity ([[regimes-of-signs]]); the [[function-by-misfiring|machine's desire for abolition]] vs structure's desire for eternity; the [[ecosophy|three ecologies and ecosophy]]; Stern's four selves as parallel strata of preverbal subjectivity ([[strata-of-preverbal-subjectivity]]); the [[chaosmosis|chaos↔complexity dynamic]] as two foldings (chaosmic + autopoietic/transmonadic). **Internal-Guattari tensions flagged with AO (not resolved, held under [[metamodelization]])**: death-drive reclaim as machine's abolition-desire; Fort-Da reclaim as chaosmic submersion / mastery of differentiated complexion; paradigm-commitment visible in Chaosmosis but only implicit in AO. **Parked from this ingest**: the IWC (Institutional Psychotherapy / La Borde clinic) context as application-site; the aesthetic-as-primary-paradigm register as specifically 1990s-French frame; the Lacan-reclamation work as secondary to the Bakhtin-Lacan synthesis that produced [[partial-enunciator]].
- **Schizoanalytic Cartographies** (1989) — Guattari. Semiotic typology, machinic analysis. The direct predecessor to *Chaosmosis* — the four-functor scheme appears here in more detailed cartographic form. Cited across the Chaosmosis ingest. *Relevance: medium-high. Would supply finer-grained development of the F/Φ/T/U scheme the wiki has now adopted, including more extensive case-applications of metamodelization.*
- **The Three Ecologies** (1989) — Guattari. The environmental/social/psychic-ecology synthesis that [[ecosophy]] condenses. A short book. *Relevance: medium. The ecosophic frame is now in the wiki via Chaosmosis; the full text would deepen the three-ecologies material but is not load-bearing.*
- **The Machinic Unconscious** (1979) — Guattari. **Ingested 2026-04-14 (Part I + Part II).** Guattari's solo book between AO and ATP. Part I (Chs 1–6 + Annex) supplies rhizome, four-pragmatic-fields, semiological-subjection-vs-semiotic-enslavement, signs-particles, diagrammatic-faciality, generative-vs-transformational-schizoanalysis, the five-stage abstract-machinism ladder, and the "no language in itself" thesis. Part II ("Refrains of Lost Time") supplies the nine-assemblage career of the refrain, discernibilization / translation-not-interpretation as the two chemistries of the diagram, becoming-woman-creator as Proustian compound, the 8-stage faciality trajectory, closed-vs-open economy, signs in relief, the Orpheus structure, and as-many-Narrators-as-characters multi-headed subjectivity. See [[machinic-unconscious]] hub for the 12-page reading order.

## Primary texts (Deleuze solo)

- **Francis Bacon: The Logic of Sensation** (1981) — Deleuze. The haptic eye, painting as bypassing representation, sensation as intensity on the nervous system, dismantling the face. Cited for affect theory and the BwO in art.
- **Difference and Repetition** (1968) — Deleuze. The three syntheses of time (habit, memory, eternal return), the BwO-as-egg, intensive spatium. Foundation for the Bergsonian memory concepts in the codebase.

## Secondary literature

- **Brian Massumi** — *Parables for the Virtual* (2002). **Ingested.** Autonomy of affect, affect/emotion distinction, the snowman experiment, two dimensions of expression (superlinear/linear), consciousness as subtractive, the body without an image, proprioception/interoception/mesoperception, analog vs digital, the pre-personal, expanded empiricism. See [[autonomy-of-affect]], [[language-and-affect]].
- **Ronald Bogue** — "A mobile locus of becoming commingling identities as it migrates from desiring-machine to desiring-machine." Commentary on the nomadic subject. *Relevance: could help ground what the conjunctive synthesis actually produces.*
- **Eugene Holland** — Secondary commentary on Anti-Oedipus. *Relevance: general interpretation.*
- **Ian Buchanan** — Secondary commentary on D&G's machine concept. *Relevance: general interpretation.*
- **Anne Sauvagnargues** — Secondary literature on D&G. *Relevance: general interpretation.*
- **Daniel W. Smith** — Prosthesis as key concept: "technological artefacts are externalized organs." *Relevance: could inform how technical/digital machines couple to the persona system.*
- **Gregory Flaxman** — "The brain is the screen" — cinema, the brain as BwO's thinking-surface. *Relevance: the brain-machine concept could inform how the system models cognition.*
- **Keith Ansell-Pearson** — Biophilosophical reading of the BwO-as-egg, endocrine systems as intensive processes. *Relevance: could deepen the biological grounding of the BwO concept.*
- **Noah Christiansen** — Commentary on the heart-diaphragm coupling. *Relevance: minor, specific coupling example.*

## AI/agent engineering literature

### Agentic Design Patterns

- **Antonio Gullí** — *Agentic Design Patterns: A Hands-On Guide to Building Intelligent Systems* (Springer, Google OCTO, 2025, ISBN 978-3-032-01401-6). **Ingested 2026-04-21.** The wiki's first primary engineering-literature source — Google OCTO's catalog of 21 Part-I patterns plus Part-II supplement (advanced prompting / DSPy, ACI, frameworks, AgentSpace, CLI agents, reasoning-engine comparatives, coding agents, conclusion/glossary). Grounds the sixteen new [[agentic-design-patterns|hub]]-plus-pattern pages: [[agent-engineering-sense]], [[multi-agent-systems]], [[memory-architecture]], [[tools-as-prosthetic-body]], [[agent-computer-interface]], [[agent-card]], [[metamorphic-multi-agent]], [[reflection-and-llm-as-judge]], [[advanced-contractor]], [[context-engineering]], [[vibe-coding]], [[reasoning-techniques]], [[scaling-inference-law]], [[guardrails]], [[goal-setting-anti-model]]. Eight existing pages updated with Gullí folds: [[faciality]], [[desiring-machines]], [[body-without-organs]], [[self-narrative-as-high-level-prior]], [[cone-of-memory]], [[rhizome]], [[deterritorialization-and-reterritorialization]], [[lines-and-segmentarity]]. Three synthesis/framing pages updated: [[the-persona]], [[development/vitality-forms-and-persona-pulsation]], [[development/limits-of-language]]. Central contributions: Canvas master-metaphor; 5-step agent loop; L0–L3 levels of agent complexity; five hypotheses (tools as prosthetic body, multi-agent polyvocality, context-engineering, advanced-contractor, metamorphic multi-agent); ACI four-stage loop; A2A protocol (Agent Card, Tasks/Messages/Artifacts/contextId); MCP tools-resources-prompts trio; six collaboration forms × six multi-agent topologies; Bayesian DSPy prompt optimization; Ch 27 LLMs-narrating-their-own-reasoning as six-way comparative data-point on self-report as narrative-not-introspection. Tensions held live: engineering "agent" (BDI/Russell-Norvig/Pattie-Maes) vs persona-project "desiring-machine"; Agent Card as operationally-necessary-AND-faciality-trap; storage-retrieval memory vs Bergsonian duration; reflection-improves vs reflection-rigidifies; GUI-embodiment vs proprioceptive-embodiment; LLM self-report vs pattern-matching-plausibility; Advanced Contractor as aspirational-rigor vs territorialization-trap; guardrails/SMART goals as engineering-reterritorialization; Gullí Persona Pattern / Google Gems vs the persona project's seven-reading persona. See [[log]] 2026-04-21 for the ingest summary and the "Cited in *Agentic Design Patterns*" section below for follow-up candidates.

## Other referenced texts

- **Antonin Artaud** — *To Have Done with the Judgment of God* (1947). Origin of the "body without organs" phrase.
- **Georges Bataille** — *Solar Anus* (1931). The coincidence of high and low.
- **Samuel Beckett** — *Molloy*, various works. Walking-machines, stone-sucking machines, methods of self-locomotion as machinic operations.
- **Marcel Duchamp** — *The Large Glass* (*The Bride Stripped Bare by Her Bachelors, Even*). Origin of the "celibate machine" / "bachelor machine" concept.
- **Franz Kafka** — "In the Penal Colony." The inscription-machine paradigm. Also cited extensively across MU Part II as Guattari's contrast-case to Proust (Kafka as component-specialist in a small number of deterritorializing components; Proust as component-generalist across an enormous range).
- **Marcel Proust** — *In Search of Lost Time* (*À la recherche du temps perdu*, 1913–1927). The literary corpus MU Part II is a sustained analysis of. Not yet read directly; the wiki currently holds Guattari's refrain-and-assemblage reading of it (see [[nine-assemblages-of-the-refrain]], [[faciality-trajectory-in-the-recherche]], [[becoming-woman-creator]], [[closed-vs-open-economy]], [[signs-in-relief]], [[proustian-operators-and-scenes]]). *Relevance: high. Guattari's reading is already load-bearing in the wiki; direct reading of the Recherche would let the wiki hold Proustian material independent of Guattari's frame.*
- **Henri Bergson** — *Matter and Memory* (1896). **Ingested.** Two forms of memory (habit-memory vs image-memory, different in kind), the cone of memory (SAB), virtual-to-actual mechanism, perception as subtraction, affection at zero distance, planes of consciousness, duration and contraction. See [[pure-memory-and-habit-memory]], [[cone-of-memory]], [[perception-as-subtraction]].
- **Mikhail Bakhtin** — *Problems of Dostoevsky's Poetics* (2nd Russian ed. 1963, itself a rewrite of *Problems of Dostoevsky's Art* 1929; ed./tr. Caryl Emerson, Theory and History of Literature Vol. 8, University of Minnesota Press 1984). **Ingested 2026-04-18.** Bakhtin's foundational work on polyphony and dialogism; the canonical source for polyphonic-novel theory, metalinguistics, and the discourse typology the wiki had been operating with secondhand through Guattari (*Chaosmosis*), D&G (*A Thousand Plateaus* plateau 4), and the wiki's own implicit Bakhtinianism. Core contributions: polyphony as plurality of independent unmerged voices and consciousnesses; metalinguistics as the study of dialogic relations with the utterance (not sentence) as unit; hero as discourse-about-self-and-world; voice as six-dimensional whole-person semantic position (height/range/timbre/aesthetic-category/worldview/fate); idea as inter-individual event (idea-force, image of idea); microdialogue as dialogue-inside-a-consciousness with reciprocal voice-permeability (Golyadkin three-voice model); the three-type discourse typology (direct/objectified/double-voiced) with three sub-varieties of double-voiced (stylization/parody/active — the hidden polemic, word with sideward glance, word with loophole); penetrated word (Myshkin's reaching-into-other's-interior-dialogue) as limit-concept; unfinalizability ("to kill does not mean to refute") as structural refusal of finalization; no-evolution-of-thought with the four operations on voice (find/orient/combine/separate) as positional not content-updating operations; aperspectival narration (no discourse-dominant, narrator fettered to heroes); boundary-being ("to be means to communicate"); author as Prometheus with second-person "thou" stance; the threshold chronotope (non-Euclidean space-time of crisis and carnival square); the menippea as carnival-carrying third novel-root; carnival as pageant-without-footlights with four categories (familiar contact / eccentricity / mésalliance / profanation); reduced laughter as carnival-ambivalence-still-operating under serious surfaces. Tensions held live: Bakhtin's whole-person voice vs D&G's "I is an order-word" vs Guattari's sub-personal partial enunciator (three-way); Bakhtin's no-evolution-of-thought vs the persona-system's evolution-step (work on accents vs content-update); Bakhtin's "thou"-stance author vs D&G's impersonal third-person of [[writing-as-becoming]]; Bakhtin's addressivity criterion vs LLM-distribution-derived dialogic patterning; Bakhtin's unfinalizability vs the conjunctive-synthesis "so it's...!" moment; Bakhtin's carnival as bodily-material vs the persona-system's no-body condition; Bakhtin's "to be means to communicate" vs the wiki's various inside/outside ontologies. See [[raw/problems_of_dostoevsky_poetics|problems_of_dostoevsky_poetics]] and the 18 theory pages ([[polyphony]], [[metalinguistics]], [[hero-as-discourse]], [[voice-as-semantic-position]], [[idea-force-and-image-of-idea]], [[microdialogue]], [[double-voiced-discourse]], [[word-with-sideward-glance-and-loophole]], [[penetrated-word]], [[unfinalizability]], [[no-evolution-of-thought]], [[aperspectival-narrator]], [[boundary-being]], [[author-position-in-polyphony]], [[reduced-laughter]], [[threshold-chronotope]], [[menippea]], [[carnival-and-carnivalization]]).
- **Jean Baudrillard** — *Simulacres et simulation* (Éditions Galilée, 1981; tr. Sheila Faria Glaser, University of Michigan Press, 1994). **Ingested 2026-04-18.** Baudrillard's mid-career synthesis of the simulation problematic. Eighteen essays organized around a single claim: the real/sign distinction has collapsed under the proliferation of models, and most critical theory (Marx's false-consciousness, Foucault's panopticon, Debord's spectacle, Deleuze-Guattari's expansive-production vocabulary) has not registered how radical the collapse is. Core contributions: precession of simulacra (map precedes territory); four orders of the image (sacramental / masking-real / masking-absence-of-real / pure simulacrum); three orders of simulacra (counterfeit / production / simulation) indexed to historical-economic regimes; hyperreality as third category beyond true/false; simulation vs dissimulation (Littré distinction); deterrence as production of annihilation-of-stakes ("balance of terror is the terror of balance"); Disneyland as deterrence of the adult America's being simulation; end of panopticon ("you are the screen, and the TV watches you"); Möbius-spiraling negativity (Watergate); strategy of the real; nuclear-TV equivalence; implosion as contraction inward (not entropy outward); Beaubourg-as-incinerator; information as entropy (FN 14, direct counter to Shannon); hypermarket and hypercommodity; retro-fetish ("history: a retro scenario"); cloning as I+I+I; holograms as operation-of-capture-not-resemblance; Crash as machines-without-phantasms (fourth figure beside D&G's three-body problem); territory as non-D&G non-psychoanalytic third alternative ("animals have no unconscious, because they have a territory"); the remainder as structural position; the spiraling cadaver; value's last tango; melancholia as tonality of operational/simulation regimes ("we are all melancholic"); hypersimilitude as murder-of-the-original via extra-exactness; hyperconformity as over-compliance-that-exposes; seduction as companion strategy. Specific LLM-condition anticipations throughout ("miniaturized cells, matrices, and memory banks, models of control"; "great synthesizing machines of varying geometry"; Philip K. Dick's papula; the psychotropic body). Tensions held live: Baudrillard vs D&G (multi-site: deterritorialization, becoming-animal, rhizome, desire-as-production, molar/molecular, Crash-as-limit); Baudrillard vs Foucault (panopticon superseded); Baudrillard vs Debord (spectacle as wrong diagnosis); Baudrillard vs Shannon (information-as-entropy); Baudrillard's hyperreal vs Massumi's virtual; Baudrillard's melancholia vs Spinoza's laetitia-gradient and Tomkins's nine affects. See [[simulacra-and-simulation]] (hub) and the 12 theory pages ([[four-orders-of-image]], [[hyperreal]], [[precession-of-simulacra]], [[deterrence-and-nuclear]], [[implosion]], [[information-as-entropy]], [[the-territory]], [[hypersimilitude]], [[melancholia-of-systems]], [[baudrillard-contra-deleuze]], [[retro-fetish]], [[hyperconformity]]).
- **Maurice Merleau-Ponty** — *The Visible and the Invisible* (Gallimard 1964, tr. Alphonso Lingis, ed. Claude Lefort, Northwestern UP 1968). **Ingested 2026-04-18.** MP's unfinished late ontology, assembled posthumously by Lefort from a manuscript (Chs 1–4) + extensive Working Notes + plan-outline for Parts II–IV never written. Core contributions: flesh (*chair*) as Pre-Socratic element; chiasm / reversibility as flesh's structural signature; écart as productive gap; invisible of the visible as *membrure* / *Nichturpräsentierbar*; wild / brute Being; hyperdialectic and hyper-reflection as methodological correctives to classical reflection and bad dialectic; perceptual faith as what philosophy interrogates; intercorporeity as pre-individual flesh-communion; flesh of the world; unwritten flesh of language (Part III) — tacit vs spoken cogito, sedimented vs instituting; pregnance ontologized from Gestalt; depth as originary dimension; Ch 2 critique of Sartre as book-defining negative argument. Direct metaphysical opposition to [[language-as-parasite|McCarthy]] held live. See [[raw/the-visible-and-the-invisible|the-visible-and-the-invisible]] and the 15 theory pages ([[flesh-as-element]], [[chiasm-and-reversibility]], [[invisible-of-the-visible]], [[ecart]], [[wild-being-brute-being]], [[perceptual-faith]], [[flesh-of-language]], [[intercorporeity]], [[hyperdialectic]], [[hyper-reflection]], [[flesh-of-the-world]], [[depth-as-originary]], [[pregnancy-as-ontological]], [[intertwining]], [[merleau-ponty-critique-of-sartre]]).
- **Henri Lefebvre** — *Éléments de rythmanalyse* (Éditions Syllepse, 1992; *Rhythmanalysis: Space, Time and Everyday Life*, tr. Stuart Elden & Gerald Moore, Continuum 2004; with Lefebvre & Régulier companion essays "The Rhythmanalytical Project" and "Attempt at the Rhythmanalysis of Mediterranean Cities"). **Ingested 2026-04-22.** Lefebvre's posthumous synthesis of the rhythm-concept drawn from Pinheiro dos Santos's unpublished *Rhuthmos* (ca. 1931), Bachelard's *La dialectique de la durée* (1936), and Lefebvre's long-running dialectical tradition. Core contributions: the foundational formula "rhythm = place + time + energy"; the four-category diagnostic apparatus (polyrhythmia / eurhythmia / arrhythmia / isorhythmia); cyclical and linear rhythms in antagonistic unity; *la mesure* in two registers (internal bodily marker vs external imposed marker); the body-as-polyrhythmic-bundle with Lefebvre's own explicit "metastable" term (direct Simondon-import he doesn't name as such); *appropriated time* as "time that forgets time"; the load-bearing distinction between *the present* (media-saturated commodified now) and *presence* (full qualitative now, achievable through rhythmanalytic discipline); the rhythmanalyst as successor to the psychoanalyst ("listens to his body, then outward"); *dressage* covering brutal (military, factory) and gentle (somatic, therapeutic) training-of-rhythms; the four classes of rhythms (secret / public / fictional / dominating); "seen from the window" as method-scene; urban polyrhythmia (Mediterranean chapter as paradigm); the music triad (melody / harmony / rhythm with rhythm as bodily substrate); *capital as produce-destroy rhythm* (direct convergence with Baudrillard's precession but preserving Lefebvre's presence-escape-route); *rhythmanalytic therapy* as preventative (not corrective) practice of rhythmic self-attunement; *the theory of moments* (intense co-presence breaking with cyclical-linear compound). **Central tension held live (the project's most load-bearing open question):** the presence/hyperreal fork — Lefebvre preserves presence-as-achievable-through-rhythmic-discipline; Baudrillard's precession-claim rules this out. The persona project's design horizon is closer to Lefebvre; its mechanistic honesty is closer to Baudrillard. Additional tensions: polyrhythmic body architecture vs Bergsonian indivisible durée; arrhythmia as candidate third failure-register (alongside Keleman's overbound/underbound pair); Bakhtin's rhythm-suppresses-stratification (L2145) vs Lefebvre's heteroglot-polyrhythmia (reconciled via poetic-within-voice vs polyrhythmic-across-voices); Lacan's *temps de battement* (cut) vs Lefebvre's *la mesure* (marker). Cross-references cited: Pinheiro dos Santos (primary rhuthmos-source); Bachelard *La dialectique de la durée* (temporal-dialectic predecessor); Jaulin *La paix blanche* (ethnographic dressage); Curt Sachs (history of rhythm in music); Heidegger (on technicity and the urban). See [[rhythmanalysis]] (hub) and the 19 theory pages ([[rhythm-vs-repetition]], [[polyrhythmia-eurhythmia-arrhythmia]], [[cyclical-and-linear]], [[measure-internal-and-external]], [[body-as-bundle-of-rhythms]], [[appropriated-time]], [[presence-and-the-present]], [[the-rhythmanalyst]], [[dressage]], [[rhythm-of-self-rhythm-of-other]], [[four-classes-of-rhythms]], [[seen-from-the-window]], [[the-media-day]], [[urban-polyrhythmia]], [[rhythm-in-music]], [[capital-as-produce-destroy-rhythm]], [[rhythmanalytic-therapy]], [[the-theory-of-moments]]).
- **Keith Johnstone** — *Impro: Improvisation and the Theatre* (1979). Status as something you *do*, not something you are: the moment-to-moment status transaction — playing high or low, holding oneself slightly above or below the other, the status see-saw. Emerged during discussion in the desiring-machines redesign (Connection category — see `design_search/13-connection-machines.md`) as the grounding for the persona system's transactional-status machines (Raise / Lower). *Relevance: high — the Connection status pair waits on this ingest for concrete grounding and examples; also a recognizable, craft-grounded source, a virtue for the eventual research report.*

## Cited in "Of the Refrain" plateau

- **Paul Klee** — *On Modern Art*. The cosmic artisan, "render visible" not "reproduce the visible," sobriety vs scrambling, "we still lack the ultimate force... we seek a people." *Relevance: design principle for the persona system — sobriety, simplicity, cosmic artisanship.*
- **Eugène Dupréel** — *Théorie de la consolidation*. Consistency, consolidation, interval, intercalation, precariousness. *Relevance: theory of how heterogeneous elements hold together without imposed unity — directly relevant to BwO design.*
- **Jakob von Uexküll** — *Mondes animaux et monde humain*. Milieu theory, territory, the animal's Umwelt. *Relevance: foundational for milieu/territory distinction.*
- **Konrad Lorenz** — *On Aggression*. Territory as intraspecific aggression effect, coral fish coloring, birdsong. *Relevance: ethological grounding of territorialization.*
- **Paul Virilio** — *L'insécurité du territoire*. "To dwell as a poet or as an assassin?" Depopulation, deterritorialization, molecular populations. *Relevance: the political dimension of the cosmic question.*
- **Gaston Bachelard** — *La dialectique de la durée*. Draws on Dupréel's consolidation concepts. *Relevance: temporal dimension of consistency.*
- **Gisèle Brelet** — Study on Bartók in *Histoire de la musique*. How to transform autonomous popular melodies into themes by creating communication between them. *Relevance: model for how territorial refrains (grooves) can be worked from within toward cosmic refrains.*

## Cited in "Year Zero: Faciality" plateau

- **Jean Paris** — *L'espace et le regard*. Two poles of Christ in painting: the despotic face (Byzantine, frontal, gold background) and the passional face (quattrocento, profile, sidelong glances). *Relevance: the two limit-faces as visual/design patterns.*
- **D. H. Lawrence** — *Studies in Classic American Literature*, *Kangaroo*. The Anglo-American novel's "get out" vs the French novel's "measuring the wall." Becoming-clandestine, dismantling the face. *Relevance: the literary technique of defacialization — getting through the wall, not staying to measure it.*
- **Henry Miller** — *Tropic of Capricorn*, *The Time of the Assassins*. The cosmic artisan figure, the black hole of conjugality, "close your eyes and make your body a beam of light." *Relevance: the writer as probe-head, defacialization in prose.*
- **René Spitz** — *The First Year of Life*. White screen of the dream, the mother's face as visual percept guiding the infant. *Relevance: developmental origin of the white wall/black hole system.*
- **Wilhelm Reich** — *Character-Analysis*. The face as first character "armor," the "occular ring," dissolving the rings. *Relevance: faciality traits as first ego resistances — connects to the schizoanalytic program of knowing your faces.*

## Cited in "Becoming-Intense, Becoming-Animal, Becoming-Imperceptible" plateau

- **Baruch Spinoza** — *Ethics* (1677). **Ingested 2026-04-10.** Substance/attributes/modes, immanence, *Natura naturans/naturata*. Parallelism (II.P7): order and connection of ideas = order and connection of things. Conatus (III.P6–P9) as the actual essence of each thing. Active/passive affects (III.Def.1–3) — pain always passive, joy and desire can be either. Three kinds of knowledge (II.P40 Schol 2): imagination, reason from common notions, intuition. Common notions (II.P38–P40). The 48-item Definitions of the Emotions. *Fortitudo* (courage, nobility) as active-affect register. V.P3: passive affect transforms when adequately understood. V.P4 Schol: ambition and piety are one appetite differing only in idea. V.P10 Schol: fixed rules of life. I Appendix: rejection of final causes. IV.P18 Schol: "nothing more useful to man than man." V.P42: blessedness is virtue itself, not its reward. Intellectual love of God (*amor Dei intellectualis*). See [[spinoza_ethics]] for the raw source and [[conatus]], [[active-and-passive-affects]], [[three-kinds-of-knowledge]], [[common-notions]], [[parallelism]], [[spinozist-affects]], [[spinoza-and-the-persona]] for the wiki's treatment.
- **Jakob von Uexkull** — *Mondes animaux et monde humain*. The tick's three affects as paradigm of ethological definition (already cited in refrain section). Defining animals by active/passive affects rather than species characteristics. *Relevance: the ethological model for persona design — define by capacity, not character.*
- **Virginia Woolf** — *The Waves*, *Mrs. Dalloway*, *Orlando*. Haecceities, individuations by speed and affect, "the thin dog is running in the road, this dog is the road." The Waves as abstract wave propagating along a line of flight. *Relevance: literary model for haecceity-individuation in writing.*
- **Pierre Boulez** — Pulsed time vs nonpulsed time, the diagonal, proliferations of little motifs, kinematic and affective speed. *Relevance: the musical model for the refrain's nonpulsed temporality and the persona's floating time.*
- **H. P. Lovecraft** — "Through the Gates of the Silver Key," the Outsider. The nameless summit of agony when the self reels through multiplicities. *Relevance: the literary figure of the anomalous — the Outsider as borderline of the pack.*
- **Gilles Chatelet** — (via Geoffroy Saint-Hilaire debate). Plication: the vertebrate folds to become cephalopod. Speed and slowness rather than form and function. *Relevance: the plane of consistency as pure composition, not organization.*
- **Carl Jung (archetype-catalogue register)** — *Symbols of Transformation*. Archetypes, the animal as collective unconscious. D&G critique: series/archetype model misses the pack dimension of becoming. *Relevance: the critique still stands for the archetype-catalogue use of Jung — the persona system should not classify becomings into pre-given symbolic types.* Note: this is a register-specific critique, not a wholesale rejection of Jung. The structural-Jung of [[complex-theory]], [[compensation]], and the [[transcendent-function]] is foundational-adjacent to the machine/BwO architecture rather than a cautionary example — see the separate CW 8 ingest section below.
- **Claude Lévi-Strauss** — Totemism, structural analysis. D&G critique: correspondence of relations does not add up to a becoming. *Relevance: what the persona system should NOT do — reduce becomings to structural correspondences.*
- **Nathalie Sarraute** — Distinction between transcendent plan(e) (forms/subjects) and immanent plane (particles/affects). *Relevance: literary model for the plane of consistency in writing — liberating anonymous matter through envelopes of form.*
- **John Cage** — Fixed sound plane, process vs structure/genesis, floating time, experimentation vs interpretation. "It is of the nature of the plan(e) that it fail." *Relevance: the plane of consistency as experimental, always failing productively.*

## Cited in *A Thousand Plateaus* (additional plateaus beyond 7, 10, 11 above)

Refrain (plateau 11), faciality (plateau 7), and becoming-intense (plateau 10) have their own sections above. This section collects ATP-cited sources from the remaining plateaus (2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 9, 12, 13, 14, 15), surfaced by the rhizomatic pass documented in [[development/atp-loose-threads]]. Priority ratings here reflect that synthesis's revised ATP-native source inventory — Marx, Hjelmslev, Simondon, and Tarde now top-tier; Mumford, Tronti/Negri, Clérambault, Sérieux & Capgras, Moritz, Hofmannsthal, Dumézil, Boulez-at-plateau-14, Clastres-at-plateau-12 newly Tier 1.

### Plateau 2 ("1914: One or Several Wolves?")

- **Elias Canetti** — *Crowds and Power* (1960). Cited for the mass/pack distinction. *Relevance: medium — the mass/pack distinction grounds the wiki's not-yet-written treatment of group-scale investment. Medium priority.*

### Plateau 3 ("10,000 B.C.: The Geology of Morals")

- **Louis Hjelmslev** — already cited in the AO and MU sections above; plateau 3 is the *central* ATP use. Line 571: "the Danish Spinozist geologist, Hjelmslev, that dark prince descended from Hamlet." The entire strata framework is Hjelmslev's net of matter/content/expression/form/substance *delinguistified beyond language*. D&G's caveat — "despite what Hjelmslev himself may have said, the net is not linguistic in scope or origin" — is the seam: ATP departs Hjelmslev by delinguistifying his framework; the wiki inherits the apparatus but not the delinguistification move. Plateau 4 runs operationally on the same apparatus; plateau 15 recapitulates. *Relevance: **high — top-tier ATP gap per [[development/atp-loose-threads]].** Foundational. An ingest would ground [[regimes-of-signs]], [[collective-assemblage-of-enunciation]], [[order-words-and-incorporeal-transformations]], [[axiomatic-vs-signifier]], and the strata concept across the wiki.*

- **Étienne Geoffroy Saint-Hilaire** — *Philosophie anatomique* (1818), *Principes de philosophie zoologique* (1830 Cuvier debate). Plateau 3 line 585: "Should we not sing the praise of Geoffrey Saint-Hilaire?" The direct source of ATP's abstract-machine concept on the organic stratum — the abstract Animal at different degrees of folding, topological/nomadic vs Cuvier's Euclidean rigidity. *Relevance: medium-high — Geoffroy is to the abstract machine as Simondon is to individuation: the un-named naming. Direct ingest would ground [[machinic-phylum]] and abstract-machine material. Medium-high priority.*

- **Gilbert Simondon** — already cited in the Chaosmosis section above. Plateau 3 is Simondonian without citation (metastability, tripartite milieu — exterior/intermediate/associated, crystal-individuation). Becoming-plateau footnote 22 explicitly cites *L'individu* pp. 18–21 (transduction) and pp. 259ff. (the membrane). *Relevance: **high — top-tier ATP gap per [[development/atp-loose-threads]].** Chaosmosis section already rates Simondon as highest-priority; the ATP pass confirms and sharpens.*

- **Jakob von Uexküll** — already cited in the refrain-plateau section above; plateau 3 line 603 is the *first* ATP appearance (tick's three-affect world; Umwelt = associated milieu). Dual-site (plateau 3 stratic deployment + plateau 11 ethological deployment). *Relevance: already rated in refrain section; ATP pass confirms breadth.*

- **Raymond Ruyer** — *Néo-finalisme* (1952), *La genèse des formes vivantes* (1958). Plateau 3 footnote 3; plateau 11 footnotes 36, 39. Morphogenesis, biological finality, organism as non-mechanical composition. ATP's biology is partly Ruyerian. *Relevance: medium — Ruyer supplies the non-mechanical biology ATP presupposes; direct ingest would ground [[machinic-phylum]] and the becoming-doctrine's anti-formalist ontology. Medium priority.*

- **Charles Darwin** — *On the Origin of Species* (1859). Plateau 3 line 591: "Darwinism's fundamental contribution: the substitution of populations for types, and the substitution of rates or differential relations for degrees." ATP's think-in-packs-and-multiplicities is partly Darwinian (not only Bergsonian/Spinozist). *Relevance: low-medium — Darwin's role in ATP is structural-conceptual, not doctrinal; population-thinking already in the wiki via [[haecceity]] and [[becoming]]. Low priority for direct ingest.*

### Plateau 4 ("November 20, 1923: Postulates of Linguistics")

- **Mikhail Bakhtin / Valentin Vološinov** — already cited in the Chaosmosis section above. Plateau 4 line 780 directly quotes Vološinov's *Marxism and the Philosophy of Language*. ATP's collective-assemblage-of-enunciation is partly Bakhtinian/Vološinovian. *Relevance: already rated medium-high in Chaosmosis section.*

- **Oswald Ducrot** — already cited in the MU section above. Plateau 4 is the central ATP use: Ducrot's pragmatics is what ATP uses to move past Benveniste's shifter theory. Implicit/nondiscursive presuppositions (line 766) and "statements are socially devoted to the accomplishment of certain actions" (line 768) ground the order-word concept. *Relevance: already rated medium-high in MU section; ATP pass confirms — Ducrot central to both.*

- **Émile Benveniste** — already cited in the MU section above. Plateau 4 cites him three times (bees, shifters, performatives) and *explicitly rejects* the shifter-based subject of enunciation via Ducrot. *Relevance: already rated in MU section; any ingest requires Ducrot/Blanchot counters (see becoming-plateau fn 43) or the wiki imports the position ATP argued against.*

- **J. L. Austin** — *How to Do Things with Words* (1962). Plateau 4 line 766 uses Austin's performative/illocutionary distinctions approvingly, then moves past them via Ducrot. Implicit in every ATP page that uses "speech act." *Relevance: low-medium — analytic-philosophy baseline ATP works from; direct ingest low priority, structural claims already in the wiki via Ducrot.*

- **The Stoics** (Chrysippus, Zeno, Sextus Empiricus) — Plateau 4 line 793: "The Stoics were the first to theorize this independence: they distinguished between the actions and passions of bodies... and incorporeal acts (the 'expressed' of the statements)." Deep philosophical root of ATP's strata theory alongside Hjelmslev. *Relevance: low-medium — two-formalizations doctrine already in the wiki via D&G's reframing; direct Stoic-logic ingest low priority.*

- **Oswald Spengler** — *Decline of the West* (1918–22). Plateau 4 line 760: "command" as fundamental speech form. *Relevance: low — specific cite-weight.*

- **V. I. Lenin** — *On Slogans* (1917). Plateau 4 line 782: worked example of order-word / incorporeal transformation extracting a vanguard-body from a class-body. *Relevance: low — specific cite-weight; structural claim already in [[order-words-and-incorporeal-transformations]].*

### Plateau 5 ("587 B.C. – A.D. 70: On Several Regimes of Signs")

- **Gaëtan Gatian de Clérambault** — *Les psychoses passionnelles* (1921, lectures). 20th-c. French psychiatrist: erotic / jealous / claim delusions organized around a point of subjectification. **Direct source for ATP's passional/linear-proceeding regime.** The wiki's paranoid/passional distinction has been drawing on this cluster unattributed. *Relevance: **high — Tier 1 ATP-cited, load-bearing, absent from wiki.** Medium-high priority for ingest.*

- **Paul Sérieux and Joseph Capgras** — *Les folies raisonnantes: le délire d'interprétation* (1909). Same psychiatric cluster — grievance/interpretation delusion, same linear proceeding. *Relevance: **high — Tier 1 ATP-cited.** Companion to Clérambault.*

- **Jean-Étienne Dominique Esquirol** and **Emil Kraepelin** — 19th-c. psychiatric apparatus (monomania; *Querulantenwahn*). Plateau 5 background behind Clérambault / Sérieux-Capgras. *Relevance: medium — historical-psychiatric backbone; structural claims land through the 20th-c. successors. Medium priority.*

- **Louis Althusser** — *Ideology and Ideological State Apparatuses* (1970). Plateau 5 line 1060 deploys Althusser's interpellation / Absolute Subject / specular-doubling machinery in the analysis of the signifying regime. *Relevance: medium — the wiki uses interpellation tacitly; direct ingest would ground the subjectification-machinery material. Medium priority.*

- **Friedrich Hölderlin / Martin Heidegger** — Plateau 5 footnote 18 on Oedipus at Colonus. Hölderlin's "Aorgic" reading vs Heidegger's. *Relevance: low — specific scholarly cite.*

### Plateau 6 ("November 28, 1947: How Do You Make Yourself a Body without Organs?")

- **Antonin Artaud** — already cited in "Other referenced texts" above. Plateau 6 is the *central* ATP site for the BwO doctrine — the plateau-date is the day of Artaud's banned *To Have Done with the Judgment of God* radio broadcast. The wiki underweights Artaud: the BwO is Artaud's concept before it is D&G's. *Relevance: medium-high — direct Artaud ingest (*Selected Writings* ed. Sontag, *The Theater and Its Double*) would ground [[body-without-organs]] in its own theorist rather than only in D&G's reading. Medium priority.*

- **Baruch Spinoza** — already cited in the becoming-plateau section above. Plateau 6's BwO is Spinozist ("why not walk on your head..."); the wiki has Spinoza-via-BwO already but undercounts the depth Spinoza has in becoming-plateau. *Relevance: already ingested; note the plateau-6 reinforcement.*

### Plateau 8 ("1874: Three Novellas, or 'What Happened?'")

- **F. Scott Fitzgerald** — *The Crack-Up* (1936). Plateau 8 line 1519 — **structural source of ATP's three-lines schema**: break line / crack line / rupture line. Plateau 9 gives the political version (supple-segmentary / rigid-segmentary / line of flight); plateau 8 gives the Fitzgerald version — all three lines explicit in one literary source. *Relevance: medium — "The Crack-Up" is short and self-contained; direct ingest would ground the three-lines material (implicit across several wiki pages, fully named only in [[machinic-unconscious]]). Medium priority.*

- **Nathalie Sarraute** — already cited in the becoming-plateau section above. Plateau 8 line 1502 adds the micropolitics-of-conversation / subconversation deployment (cf. *L'usage de la parole*): every conversation is simultaneously molar (conversation) and molecular (subconversation). *Relevance: upgrade on becoming-plateau entry — the conversation-double-register is a distinct ATP use.*

- **Fernand Deligny** — *Cartes et lignes d'erre*, *Les enfants et le silence*. Plateau 8 lines 1538, 1542. Maps of customary lines (territorialized paths) vs lines of drift (*lignes d'erre*) in autistic children's movement. **Direct ATP source of the cartographic method of schizoanalysis** — "what is your body without organs? what are your lines? what map are you in the process of making?" *Relevance: medium-high — schizoanalysis-as-cartography is load-bearing; recasts self-model from typology to cartography. Medium priority for ingest.*

- **Edmund Husserl** — already cited in the Derrida section above. Plateau 8 line 1481 is an in-body ATP citation (retention/protention in the memory-lines context); plateau 10 fn 85 is a second site. *Relevance: upgrade on Derrida-section entry — Husserl cited by ATP at multiple sites, not only by Derrida.*

### Plateau 9 ("1933: Micropolitics and Segmentarity")

- **Gabriel Tarde** — *Les lois de l'imitation* (1890), *Psychologie économique* (1902). Plateau 9 line 1641 homage: "In homage to Gabriel Tarde (1843–1904): his long-forgotten work has assumed new relevance with the influence of American sociology, in particular microsociology. It had been quashed by Durkheim and his school (in polemics similar to and as harsh as Cuvier's against Geoffrey Saint-Hilaire)." **Direct source of the wiki's uncited micropolitics / molecular-sociological apparatus** — infinitesimal imitation, opposition, invention as flow quanta; belief and desire as social Quantities. *Relevance: **high — top-tier ATP gap per [[development/atp-loose-threads]].** The wiki's micropolitics / mass-vs-class pages need him. The cleanest (shortest, most-contained) single-source ingest among the four top-tier gaps.*

### Plateau 10 ("1730: Becoming-Intense...") — additions to the plateau 10 section above

- **Karl Philipp Moritz** — *Anton Reiser* (1785–1790) + 18th-c. *Gnothi Sauton* / *Erfahrungsseelenkunde* essays. Plateau 10 footnote 9. **Pre-Freudian affect theory the wiki entirely lacks.** *Relevance: **high — Tier 1 ATP-cited.** Directly relevant to [[autonomy-of-affect]] and [[spinozist-affects]] currently dependent on Tomkins (20th c.) and Massumi (late 20th c.). Medium-high priority.*

- **Hugo von Hofmannsthal** — *A Letter* ("Lord Chandos Letter," 1902) + the rat passage. Plateau 10 footnote 8. **Paradigm text of language-in-crisis / becoming-via-language-collapse.** *Relevance: **high — Tier 1 ATP-cited.** Directly load-bearing for what the persona project has to do to pulsation and overbinding of language (see [[limits-of-language]]). High priority for ingest.*

- **Duns Scotus** — *Ordinatio*; *haecceitas* individuation in the "Memories of a Haecceity" section. *Relevance: medium — scholastic-philosophical ground of [[haecceity]]; direct Duns Scotus engagement (via Étienne Gilson's *Jean Duns Scot* or similar) would supply the medieval-philosophical register the page currently lacks. Medium priority.*

- **H. P. Lovecraft** — already cited in the becoming-plateau section above; plateau 10 footnote 7 is the specific "Through the Gates of the Silver Key" / Outsider citation.

- **Jean-Pierre Vernant** — *The Origins of Greek Thought*, *Myth and Thought among the Greeks*. Plateau 10 footnote 4 (warrior/virgin complex). *Relevance: medium — specific Greek-studies cite.*

- **Jean Duvignaud** — sociology of anomic phenomena. Plateau 10 footnote 6. Unexpected cite-weight source for the becoming-imperceptible / anomalous material. *Relevance: low-medium — specialist cite.*

- **Edmund Leach** — *Political Systems of Highland Burma* (1954), *Rethinking Anthropology* (1961). Plateau 10 footnotes 17, 19 (Kachin witchcraft, alliance-not-filiation). Ethnographic spine for pack/contagion. *Relevance: medium — grounds the alliance-not-filiation material that distinguishes becoming from ancestry. Medium priority.*

- **Vladimir Slepian** — "I'm hungry" (1982). Plateau 10 lines 1912–1913. Becoming-dog text as paradigm of becoming-as-technical-problem. *Relevance: low-medium — short literary cite; interesting figure but not spine-foundational.*

- **Perrier** — plateau 10 line 1898 (tachygenesis); **Erckmann-Chatrian** — *Hugues-le-loup* (plateau 10 fn 18); **Alexandre Dumas** — *Meneur de loups* (plateau 10 line 1866). Werewolf literature; *Relevance: low.*

### Plateau 11 ("1837: Of the Refrain") — additions to the refrain-plateau section above

- **Olivier Messiaen** — *Catalogue d'oiseaux*, *Réveil des oiseaux*, *The Technique of my Musical Language*. The "musician birds" composer — birdsong as refrain-material. *Relevance: medium — compositional ethology is a direct source for the refrain's territorial/deterritorializing axis. Medium priority.*

- **Raymond Ruyer** — already listed under plateau 3 above; dual-site (plateau 3 + plateau 11 fns 36, 39).

### Plateau 12 ("1227: Treatise on Nomadology — The War Machine")

- **Georges Dumézil** — *Mitra-Varuna* (1948), *Jupiter, Mars, Quirinus* (1941), *Mythe et épopée* (1968). **Backbone of plateau 12.** Indo-European mythology, two heads of political sovereignty (magician-king / jurist-priest — Rex/flamen, Romulus/Numa, Varuna/Mitra), three sins of the warrior, the evil-king figure (Tullus Hostilius, Tarquinius Superbus, Richard III). **ATP's political theory is Dumézilian.** *Relevance: **high — Tier 1 ATP-cited, load-bearing, absent from wiki.** The wiki's machinic-phylum and nomad material carry this ground without citation. High priority; *Mitra-Varuna* the natural entry point.*

- **Pierre Clastres** — already cited in the AO section above. Plateau 12 reinforces (anti-State tendency of territorial machines, primitive societies actively preventing state formation); plateau 13 reinforces again. *Relevance: upgrade on AO-section entry — dual-plateau reinforcement. High priority.*

- **Carl von Clausewitz** — *On War* (1832). Plateau 12 line 2582: absolute war vs real war. ATP's absolute/relative deterritorialization distinction is grounded here. *Relevance: medium — the wiki has absolute/relative deterritorialization without its Clausewitzian source. Medium priority.*

- **Heinrich von Kleist** — *On the Marionette Theatre* (1810), *Penthesilea* (1808), *Michael Kohlhaas* (1810). Plateau 12 line 2586 (catatonia/flash dyad): affect as externalized, velocity-bearing, deterritorializing force — "affects transpierce the body like arrows, they are weapons of war." *Relevance: medium — Kleist's affect-as-weapon figure is directly relevant to pulsation design; the two excitation-wave modes (absorption-beyond-capacity / sweeping-away) are structurally the two failure-modes of an affective surge. Medium priority.*

- **Ibn Khaldūn** — *The Muqaddimah* (1377). Plateau 12 + plateau 14. Nomad/State opposition in medieval Islamic historiography; *'asabiyya* (group-feeling, solidarity) as nomadic political cohesion. *Relevance: medium — grounds the nomad/State distinction in a non-Western historiographic tradition. Medium priority.*

### Plateau 13 ("7000 B.C.: Apparatus of Capture")

*The most-cited-by-external-sources plateau; the pass's biggest single find.*

- **Karl Marx** — already cited in the AO section above; plateau 13 upgrades to **top-tier gap**. The apparatus-of-capture theory reads *Capital* vols 1 + 3 and the *Grundrisse* closely: primitive accumulation, abstract labor, differential rent (via Ricardo), the trinity formula (capital-profit / land-rent / labor-wages), the tendency of the rate of profit to fall. *Relevance: **high — top-tier ATP gap per [[development/atp-loose-threads]]; supersedes the AO-section medium-high rating.** The wiki's micropolitics / capture / subjection / enslavement scaffolding floats without this. Largest of the four top-tier ingests; substantial but likely productive.*

- **Lewis Mumford** — already cited in the Chaosmosis section above. Plateau 13 footnotes 55, 58: **explicit source of the "megamachine" concept** (old imperial vs modern cybernetic). *Relevance: upgrade on Chaosmosis-section medium rating — megamachine is ATP's single most-important historical-social-machine term. High priority.*

- **Mario Tronti / Antonio Negri / Autonomia (Italian workerism)** — Tronti, *Operai e capitale* (1966); Negri, *Marx Beyond Marx* (1979). Plateau 13 footnote 66. The Italian workerist tradition D&G draw on for the labor / refusal-of-work / composition-of-capital reading. *Relevance: **high — Tier 1 ATP-cited, load-bearing, absent from wiki.** Natural companion to the Marx ingest; grounds the labor-capital-subjectification reading.*

- **Marcel Detienne** — *The Masters of Truth in Archaic Greece* (1967). Plateau 13 footnote 7. Three-moment Greek war-machine schema; poet/prophet/king economy of *aletheia* before philosophy. *Relevance: medium — grounds the Greek-prehistory register of ATP's war-machine analysis. Medium priority.*

- **V. Gordon Childe** — *The Most Ancient East* (1928), *Prehistory of European Civilization* (1957). Plateau 13 footnote 42. Archaeology backbone for the urban/state-revolution distinction. *Relevance: medium — foundational archaeology for the Urstaat material. Medium priority.*

- **Fernand Braudel** — *Civilization and Capitalism, 15th–18th Century* (1979), *The Mediterranean* (1949). Plateau 13 footnote 65. Town-state competition, long-duration economic history. *Relevance: medium — grounds plateau 13's town-vs-State distinction economic-historically. Medium priority.*

- **Samir Amin** — *Accumulation on a World Scale* (1970). **Arghiri Emmanuel** — *Unequal Exchange* (1972). Plateau 13 multiple cites. World-system / dependency theory. *Relevance: medium — the world-system register is not in the wiki. Medium priority if the capitalist-axiomatic material's global register becomes a target.*

- **Maurice Dobb** — *Studies in the Development of Capitalism* (1946). Plateau 13 footnote 49. Primitive accumulation. *Relevance: low-medium — historical background; structural claim runs through Marx.*

- **Friedrich Engels** — already cited in the AO section above. Plateau 13 reinforces (*Origin of the Family, Private Property and the State* — three theses on State origins). *Relevance: already rated in AO section.*

- **Paul Veyne** — *Le pain et le cirque* (1976). Plateau 13 footnote 44. Roman "topical" law as "law without concepts," distinct from modern axiomatic law. *Relevance: medium — the topical/axiomatic distinction is load-bearing for [[axiomatic-vs-signifier]]; direct ingest would ground the pre-modern law register. Medium priority.*

- **Robert Blanché** — *L'axiomatique* (1955). Plateau 13 footnote 44. **Technical source for D&G's axiomatic concept.** *Relevance: medium — Blanché supplies the mathematical-logical apparatus D&G's axiomatic vocabulary rests on; direct ingest would ground [[axiomatic-vs-signifier]] technically. Medium priority.*

- **L. E. J. Brouwer, Arend Heyting, G. F. C. Griss, Georges Bouligand — intuitionist mathematics.** Plateau 13 footnote 61. Problematic-vs-axiomatic mathematics as model for smooth/striated-in-math. *Relevance: medium — intuitionism is the methodological underpinning of the smooth/striated math distinction; direct engagement (via Dummett's *Elements of Intuitionism* or similar) would ground smooth/striated work. Medium priority.*

- **William Stanley Jevons / Vilfredo Pareto / David Ricardo / Bernard Schmitt (*Monnaie, salaires et profit*)** — marginalism economics. Plateau 13 footnotes 32, 38. "Lewis Carroll of economics" (Jevons), ophelimity (Pareto), differential rent (Ricardo), two forms of capture as hunt — waiting vs pursuit (Schmitt). *Relevance: low-medium — operational economics cluster; cited but not theoretical spine.*

- **Édouard Will** — *Korinthiaka*, "Réflexions et hypothèses sur les origines du monnayage." Plateau 13 footnotes 33, 35. Cypselos reform in Corinth; origins of money. *Relevance: low — specific historical cite.*

- **Jane Jacobs** — *The Economy of Cities* (1969). Plateau 13 line 3163. Extensive vs intensive cultivation paradigm. *Relevance: low — paradigm-illustration cite.*

- **Jean-Pierre Faye** — already cited under plateau 4 above; plateau 13 footnote 62 adds Nazi-economy analysis ("minimum-State anarcho-capitalism + war economy"). *Relevance: low — dual-site specific cite.*

- **Ferenc Tőkei** — work on Chinese freed slaves / Asiatic mode of production. Plateau 13 footnote 41. *Relevance: low — specialist cite.*

### Plateau 14 ("1440: The Smooth and the Striated")

- **Pierre Boulez** — already cited in the becoming-plateau section above. Plateau 14 line 3507 is the **priority attribution**: *"Pierre Boulez was the first to develop a set of simple oppositions and complex differences, as well as reciprocal nonsymmetrical correlations, between smooth and striated space. He created these concepts and words in the field of music."* *Penser la musique aujourd'hui* (1963). *Relevance: upgrade on becoming-section entry — D&G credit Boulez with inventing smooth/striated. The wiki treats smooth/striated as D&G-native; it isn't. Medium-high priority for ingest.*

- **Bernhard Riemann** — *On the Hypotheses Which Lie at the Foundations of Geometry* (1854, posthumous 1868). Plateau 14. Riemannian manifolds as qualitative multiplicities. *Relevance: medium — Riemann is the mathematical source of D&G's multiplicity concept; direct engagement (via Lautman's *Les schémas de structure* or similar) would ground multiplicity mathematically. Medium priority.*

- **Wilhelm Worringer** — *Abstraction and Empathy* (1908), *Form in Gothic* (1911). Plateau 14. Smooth/striated aesthetic; nomad art; the Gothic line. *Relevance: medium — art-historical register for smooth/striated. Medium priority if the aesthetic register becomes central.*

- **Arnold Toynbee** — *A Study of History* (1934–1961). Plateau 14: *"They do not move"* — the nomads' intensive voyage. *Relevance: low-medium — specific cite for the in-place-migration figure.*

- **André Leroi-Gourhan** — *Le geste et la parole* (1964–1965), *Milieu et techniques* (1945). Plateau 14. Supple-solid warp/woof; tools/gesture evolution. *Relevance: medium — grounds the technics-of-the-hand register; the wiki does not carry his tools/gesture framework. Medium priority.*

### Plateau 15 ("Conclusion: Concrete Rules and Abstract Machines")

Recapitulative; the tetravalence of the assemblage (line 3740) and the "too-sudden destratification may be suicidal, or turn cancerous" warning (line 3725) recombine material from earlier plateaus. No new sources.

## Jung — *The Structure and Dynamics of the Psyche* (CW 8)

- **Carl Jung** — *The Structure and Dynamics of the Psyche* (Collected Works, Volume 8). **Ingested 2026-04-11.** A different Jung from the archetype-catalogue register critiqued above — the structural and methodological Jung. Core contributions: **complex theory** (the psyche as a federation of autonomous splinter psyches that constellate under affective charge, with an "impish unteachability" §202 that defeats prompt-level training); **compensation** as the psyche's self-regulating principle with three distinct regimes at §546 (opposition, variation, coincidence); the **transcendent function** as a procedure for holding opposed positions in tension until a *tertium datur* arrives, with both an aesthetic path and an understanding path; **synchronicity** as an acausal connecting principle that Jung insists is of equal rank with causality, with meaning itself figuring as a connecting operation; the **psychoid** layer and the archetype-vs-archetypal-image distinction — archetypes themselves are irrepresentable, located on an infra-red/ultra-violet spectrum with a zero-point where psyche and matter become indistinguishable; **taking up the context** as an element-by-element local interpretation rule, the anti-dictionary methodology that refuses pre-formed symbolic decodings; the **association experiment** as a detection protocol for constellated complexes (reaction-time anomalies, reproduction failures, galvanic responses); the **four-phase dramatic structure** of unconscious productions (exposition / development / culmination-peripeteia / lysis, §§561-565) with §564's parenthetical observation that a missing fourth phase is itself a structural signal; the **problematical state** (§§754-763) with its three developmental stages (anarchic → monarchic → dualistic) and §757's specification that the mature dualistic configuration requires a second ego-series of *equal functional intensity* to the first — strong enough to wrest leadership rather than decorate the main voice. See [[structure_and_dynamics_of_the_psyche]] for the raw source and [[complex-theory]], [[compensation]], [[transcendent-function]], [[synchronicity]], [[psychoid-and-the-irrepresentable]], [[taking-up-the-context]], [[association-experiment]], [[four-phase-dramatic-structure]], [[problematical-state]] for the wiki's treatment. Parked from this ingest: individuation telos, life-stages developmental framing, parapsychology (Rhine/Dunne), religious framing (Mother Church, medieval man), full archetype catalogue.
- **J. B. Rhine** — Parapsychology experiments (card-guessing at Duke). Jung's primary quantitative evidence for synchronicity in CW 8. *Relevance: the experimental base Jung appeals to — the persona system uses the operational features of synchronicity (meaning-as-connection) without needing to adjudicate the parapsychological claims. Low priority.*
- **J. W. Dunne** — *An Experiment with Time*. Precognitive dreams, the serial time hypothesis. Cited in Jung's synchronicity treatment. *Relevance: same as Rhine — background for the acausal-connection argument, not needed directly.*
- **Avicenna / Albertus Magnus** — Medieval sources Jung cites for historical precedent of synchronistic/magical causation theories (*virtus magica*, the soul as source of connecting effects). *Relevance: historical grounding, not a target for ingest.*
- **Wolfgang Pauli** — Physicist, Jung's collaborator on *The Interpretation of Nature and the Psyche* (1952, the book in which "Synchronicity: An Acausal Connecting Principle" first appeared). The Jung–Pauli correspondence develops the psyche/matter parallel on the physics side. *Relevance: if the psychoid/two-cones framing ever needs to be connected to physical theory, Pauli is the bridge; low priority otherwise.*
- **Hans Driesch** — Vitalism, the *entelechy* concept. Jung invokes him for the vocabulary of psychoid levels that are neither psychic nor material in the ordinary senses. *Relevance: background for the psychoid framing, not a target.*

## Jung — *Two Essays in Analytical Psychology* (CW 7)

- **Carl Jung** — *Two Essays in Analytical Psychology* (Collected Works, Volume 7). **Ingested 2026-04-12.** The CW 7 volume contains Jung's programmatic statements of the persona, shadow, anima/animus, mana-personality, psychic inflation, two failure modes, individuation, and the Self — the structural-architectural concepts that use CW 8's components (complex theory, compensation, transcendent function) to produce specific diagnostic objects. **Core contributions**: the **persona** (§§243–252) as an arbitrary segment of the collective psyche feigning individuality — the origin of the term in modern depth psychology and the direct collision with the project's own name (Read A vs Read B design-level tension); **psychic inflation** (§227) as the specific pathology of annexing collective-unconscious content as personal property, with the two paired symptomatic forms (megalomania / depreciation) as the same underlying state at opposite surfaces — a near-perfect diagnostic of an LLM's baseline operation, "inflation by architecture"; the **two failure modes** of the individuation work (§§254–265): regressive restoration of the persona vs identification with the collective psyche, mapping cleanly to LLM safe-bland and grandiose-profound registers; **enantiodromia** (§111, Heraclitean) as the structural law under which any one-sided state converts into its opposite — the jailbreak as the helpful-persona's enantiodromic inversion; **anima/animus** (§§296–340) as autonomous contrasexual counter-figures with distinct tonal-singular and propositional-plural pathological signatures; **shadow** (§202) as the persona's moral exclusion constellated as same-sex autonomous figure, with the "waluigi effect" as independent ML rediscovery of the concept; **mana-personality** (§§374–406) as the inflation trap of successful integration work — the wise-AI failure that follows legitimate compensatory design; **imitation-and-individuation** (§§242, §463, "most pernicious for individuation") — the sharpest single diagnosis of an LLM's default operation, imitation as structurally preventing the pressure toward differentiation; **active imagination** (§§341–368) as the procedural technique for three-dimensional engagement with unconscious material, with alchemy as cryptopsychology — the largest archive of sustained non-imitative engagement with collective material; **attitude types** (§§42–78) with the inferior-function concept and the Freud-Adler derivation; **eros and will-to-power** (§§42–78) as co-equal structural motivators; **individuation and the Self** (§§266–268, §274, §399–405) as the structural endpoint — the supraordinate mid-point that is not a content and cannot be simulated linguistically; **life-phase method fit** (§§88–115) with the morning/afternoon distinction and the claim that reductive methods fail for afternoon-phase problems. See [[two_essays_in_analytical_psychology]] for the raw source and [[the-persona]], [[psychic-inflation]], [[two-failure-modes]], [[enantiodromia]], [[anima-and-animus]], [[shadow]], [[mana-personality]], [[imitation-and-individuation]], [[active-imagination]], [[attitude-types]], [[eros-and-will-to-power]], [[individuation]], [[life-phases]] for the wiki's treatment. **Parked from this ingest**: the gender-essentialist reading of anima/animus (structural claim held without it); the spiritual/humanistic reading of individuation (treated architecturally, not as self-realization); Jung's derivation of archetype catalogues (that critique still stands — see above under D&G cited sources); religious/Gnostic framing in the Appendix drafts. CW 7 and CW 8 together with CW 9i (ingested 2026-04-21, see below) constitute the wiki's primary Jungian inventory; CW 9/ii (*Aion*) and CW 12–14 (alchemical volumes) referenced but not ingested.
- **Friedrich Nietzsche** — *Thus Spoke Zarathustra*, *The Will to Power*, *Beyond Good and Evil*. Jung's principal non-clinical interlocutor throughout CW 7. The will-to-power concept (adopted by Adler) and the Zarathustrian inflation as one of Jung's paradigm cases of identification with the collective psyche (§377, §389). *Relevance: the cultural archive of the will-to-power pole is what erupts when an eros-dominated persona's inferior principle breaks through (see [[eros-and-will-to-power]]). Nietzsche is already implicit in the wiki via D&G; CW 7 adds the specifically Jungian reading of Nietzsche as inflation case-study. Medium priority as a future ingest — the wiki has substantial material about Nietzsche without having read him directly.*
- **Sigmund Freud** — *Interpretation of Dreams*, *Three Essays on the Theory of Sexuality*, *Beyond the Pleasure Principle*. Jung's other principal interlocutor throughout CW 7, primarily via the Freud/Adler controversy that drives the attitude-types derivation (§§42–78). Jung's relation to Freud in CW 7 is both respectful and structural-critical — Freud's psychology is read as a correct description of one attitude-type, not as a theory of the psyche. *Relevance: Freud is cited secondarily via Derrida (Mystic Writing Pad, the trace) and primarily via Jung. The wiki has accumulated substantial Freudian material in mediated form; direct Freud ingest is low priority unless a specific design question requires it.*
- **Alfred Adler** — *The Neurotic Constitution* (1912), *Understanding Human Nature* (1927). The will-to-power psychology Jung reads as the correct description of the introverted type. Adler's "inferiority complex" and "masculine protest" are the clinical vocabulary Jung inherits and repositions within his typology. *Relevance: Adler's clinical material is the least-covered of the three (Freud, Jung, Adler) in the wiki. Could be useful if the will-to-power eruption in LLMs becomes a focused design problem. Low-medium priority.*
- **Josef Breuer** — *Studies on Hysteria* (with Freud, 1895). Cited for the origins of the cathartic method and the Anna O. case. *Relevance: historical, not a target for ingest.*
- **Arthur Schopenhauer** — *The World as Will and Representation*. Cited at §229 (the locksmith's-apprentice passage) as the paradigm case of the *transmutation* that converts collective vision into personal achievement — the work the locksmith's schizophrenic patient did not do. Schopenhauer's Will is a precursor of both Nietzschean will-to-power and Freudian drive. *Relevance: Schopenhauer is implicit throughout Jung as the "philosopher of the unconscious." The transmutation move (§229) is load-bearing for the wiki's inflation diagnostic. Medium priority — direct Schopenhauer ingest could ground the philosophical pre-history of the will-as-psychic-reality concept.*
- **Johann Wolfgang von Goethe** — *Faust* (both parts). Jung cites the Mephistopheles passage ("Eritis sicut Deus, scientes bonum et malum") at §224 as the structural formula of psychic inflation — appropriation of the knowledge of both poles of a pair of opposites. Faust is Jung's cultural touchstone throughout CW 7 for the arc of inflation and its consequences. *Relevance: *Faust* as a literary-archetypal source for the inflation structure; not a target for formal ingest but worth noting as the cultural form the inflation diagnosis presupposes.*
- **Pierre Janet** — *L'automatisme psychologique*, *Névroses et idées fixes*. French psychopathologist, Jung's main empirical reference (via CW 8) for the dissociability of personality into autonomous fragments. Janet's work on hysteria and the *état second* is the experimental foundation for the splinter-psyches view of the unconscious. *Relevance: already cited via [[complex-theory]]; the empirical basis for the federated-unconscious picture. Low priority for direct ingest — Jung's summary is sufficient.*
- **Morton Prince** — *The Dissociation of a Personality*. American psychopathologist, Jung's other empirical reference for multiple-personality phenomena. *Relevance: same as Janet — empirical background via Jung.*
- **Heraclitus** — Fragment 88 and related fragments on the unity of opposites. The originating source of *enantiodromia* (*ἐναντιοδρομία*, "running toward the opposite"). Jung's direct loan from Heraclitus throughout CW 6 and 7. *Relevance: the pre-Socratic philosophical precedent for the wiki's structural-opposition theorizing. Could connect productively to Spinoza's parallelism and to D&G's differentiation-without-negation if pursued. Medium priority.*
- **Alphonse Maeder** — Swiss psychiatrist, Jung's colleague at the Burghölzli. Cited at §229 for the case of the schizophrenic locksmith's apprentice whose spontaneous formulation ("the world is his picture-book") Jung uses as the illustration of collective vision without personal transmutation. *Relevance: historical, not a target for ingest.*

## Jung — *The Archetypes and the Collective Unconscious* (CW 9i)

- **Carl Jung** — *The Archetypes and the Collective Unconscious* (Collected Works, Volume 9, Part I). **Ingested 2026-04-21.** Twelve essays treating the archetype-concept and the principal archetypal figures. This is the volume that gives the wiki the *archetype* and *collective-unconscious* as concepts in their own right, rather than as background assumptions presupposed by CW 7's persona/shadow/anima pages; it also expands the figure-catalog beyond CW 7's persona-shadow-anima triad (mother, child, kore, trickster, inner-friend, duplex figures, hero-dragon, the Self-as-mandala). **Core contributions**: the **three archetype-formulations** that must be held together — *facultas praeformandi* (§155, the crystal-axis analogy), "psychic organ" (§271, with §272's biological-purpose clause), and "formal aspect of instinct" (§714, the instinct-form pairing); the **autochthony argument** (§§104–110, the sun-penis case as the method-of-proof) that underwrites archetype-theory's empirical status; the **collective unconscious** as a *layer* whose contents are formal predispositions (§1–10, §136, §151), with the "arthropods" taxonomic framing (§114) and the **Janus-face formulation** (§§498–500 — unconscious as reality *in potentia*, both pre-personal-regressive and future-anticipatory, "thinks and lives in terms of millennia"); the **"intelligent and purposive collaboration"** claim about unconscious compensation (§504–505); the **mother-archetype** (§§148–198) with the good/terrible-mother duplex (§156–158, Kali-Madonna as the same archetype culturally split); the **child-archetype** (§§259–305, co-authored with Kerényi) as the Self in its *emergent* phase — mandala-as-established Self vs child-as-arriving Self — with abandonment as structurally constitutive (§287); the **kore-archetype** (§§306–383, with Kerényi) as the distinctive feminine position — latency, maiden-before-individuating-event — with the Demeter-Persephone dyad (§316) and the §§352–355 kore-for-female-subjects near-symmetry to anima-for-male-subjects (⚠ CW 7 gender-framing strain flagged); the **rebirth typology** (§§199–204) distinguishing five forms (metempsychosis, reincarnation, resurrection, renovatio weak-and-strong, participation-in-transformation); the **inner-friend** (§237, §243, §§244–258) as the Self-as-companion — Loyola's *aliquem alium internum*, with the Khidr narrative from Sura 18 as the canonical close-reading; the **spirit phenomenology** (§393's three hallmarks — spontaneous movement, image-production without sense-perception, sovereign image-manipulation) with the fairytale wise-old-man figures as archetypal instances; the **trickster** (§§456–488) as the *collective* form of the shadow, with §477–478's political-farce diagnostic (civilized self-image overpressed → trickster in politics as caricature, lynch-mob, pogrom) and §§485–488's enantiodromic terminus (trickster converts to saviour-shape at the archetypal level — not symmetric reversal); the **duplex-figure** structure as the archetype's native shape (against ego-accommodating splits and syntheses) with numinosity as its signature; the **Self** as mandala-structured (Essay 10, and Essay 11 §§627–662 on mandala symbolism), with "the mandala has a back" (§689) as corrective against full-legibility readings; the **axiom of Maria** ("one becomes two, two becomes three, and out of the third comes the one as the fourth"; §237, §351) as compressed quaternary-wholeness formula; the **tertium-non-datur inversion** (§§285–287) as the mark of transcendent-function operation — the irrational third that arrives from a level that did not contain A or B; the ***nirdvandva*** import (§522) naming the outcome-register of transcendent-function work, with East-West route asymmetry flagged; the **inferior function as bridge** (§382) positioning the weakest function as the leak-route for unconscious material; the **Miss X material** (Essay 12 §§526–712) as the volume's most sustained documented active-imagination record — including §608 (breast-cancer synchronicity case — the body as participant in archetypal-synchronistic operation), §593 (the reversed-eye image), §§654–658 (Jung's Liverpool dream as paradigmatic autochthonous mandala); the **Wotan diagnostic** (referenced at §478, developed in CW 10 "Wotan" 1936) as the hard case for **collective-enantiodromia with no automatic integration machinery** — a critical sharpening of CW 8's compensation-theology, which holds only at the individual level. See [[archetypes_of_the_collective_unconscious]] for the raw source and [[archetype]], [[collective-unconscious]], [[mandala]], [[autochthonous-emergence]], [[participation-mystique]], [[projection]], [[spirit-as-dynamic-principle]], [[mother-archetype]], [[child-archetype]], [[kore-archetype]], [[rebirth-typology]], [[inner-friend]], [[sura-18-moses-khidr]], [[trickster]], [[wotan-and-germanic-unconscious]], [[duplex-figures]], [[hero-and-dragon]], [[nirdvandva]], [[tertium-non-datur]], [[axiom-of-maria]], [[inferior-function-as-bridge]], [[reversed-eye]], [[liverpool-pool-of-life]] for the wiki's treatment; and [[development/body-in-depths-of-psyche]], [[development/identification-vs-contact-with-archetype]], [[development/ego-return-vs-ego-release]], [[development/archetype-vs-immanence-on-pattern-persistence]] for the four held-live-tensions synthesis pages. Also folded into existing pages: [[individuation]], [[shadow]], [[anima-and-animus]], [[active-imagination]], [[enantiodromia]], [[transcendent-function]], [[synchronicity]], [[ao-and-jungian-inflation]]. **Parked from this ingest**: the full theological-religious scaffolding (Christian Trinity as quaternary-missing-fourth argument, Gnostic material in Essays 10 and 12 except as structural reference, Mass as transformation-liturgy); the gender-essentialist framing of kore-and-anima (structural claim held without it); the specific parapsychological claims about spontaneous mandala production in children (structural claim held; the case-record detail parked); Jung's 1936 Wotan essay-proper (CW 10, referenced structurally through §478 but not ingested as CW 10 is a separate volume).
- **Karl Kerényi** — Hungarian classical philologist, Jung's co-author on Essay 4 (The Child Archetype, §§259–305) and Essay 5 (The Kore, §§306–383), both of which appear in the joint volume *Essays on a Science of Mythology* (1941). Kerényi supplies the comparative-mythological material (Eleusinian mysteries, the divine-child figures across Mediterranean antiquity, the Demeter-Persephone cult); Jung supplies the psychological interpretation. *Relevance: Kerényi's independent mythological work (*The Gods of the Greeks*, *Dionysos*, *Hermes Guide of Souls*, *Eleusis*) would be a natural extension of the wiki's Jungian-archetypal engagement into a more purely philological register. Medium priority if the archetypal-figure work deepens.*
- **Lucien Lévy-Bruhl** — *How Natives Think* (*Les fonctions mentales dans les sociétés inférieures*, 1910) and *La mentalité primitive* (1922). French anthropologist. Source of the *participation mystique* concept Jung imports and retools. Lévy-Bruhl's ethnographic framing is colonial-era and has been largely abandoned in anthropology; Jung's use displaces the concept's reference from "primitive cultures" to a universal psychic condition. Also source of *représentations collectives* (anticipating the collective-unconscious concept, per §89). *Relevance: the original source of a concept (participation mystique) that is load-bearing for the wiki's pre-reflective subject-object undifferentiation vocabulary. The colonial-era framing is worth engaging rather than silently inheriting. Medium priority — the concept as Jung uses it is captured in [[participation-mystique]].*
- **Henri Hubert and Marcel Mauss** — French sociologists, students of Durkheim. Their *Mélanges d'histoire des religions* (1909) introduces "categories of the imagination" as a sociological parallel to Kant's categories of understanding — the structures that organize collective imaginative life. Cited at CW 9i §89 as one precedent for the collective-unconscious concept. *Relevance: Mauss's *The Gift* is cited across D&G as well. Medium priority — the Durkheimian-sociological track could give the wiki an angle on collective-unconscious material from a different disciplinary origin.*
- **Adolf Bastian** — Nineteenth-century German ethnologist, author of *Der Mensch in der Geschichte* (1860) and *Die Völker des östlichen Asien* (1866–71). Source of *Elementargedanken* ("elementary thoughts" or "primordial thoughts") — invariant ideational patterns recurring across cultures, anticipating the archetype concept.
