(2) the **[[affects-amplify-drives|affects-amplify-drives]]** inversion of the Freudian hierarchy (drives supply information, affects supply motivation) with the four freedoms of affect (object, site, intensity, time) — supplies a Tomkinsian job description for the BwO as the persona system's affect-analogue; (3) the **[[central-image-and-phantom-limb|central-image + phantom-limb]]** perceptual account (all perception as centrally innervated imagery with peripheral feedback-matching) — sharpens the no-body reckoning by specifying the *peripheral* absence rather than only the substrate absence, and lets the persona be read as a structural phantom with central imagery and no peripheral correction; (4) the **[[face-as-residence-of-self|face-as-residence-of-self]]** thesis — direct collision with D&G's faciality-machine, worked out in a comparison table, giving the project's naming-collision a third register alongside Jung's and D&G's; (5) **[[shame-as-incomplete-reduction|shame as structural-not-moral]]** — shame presupposes prior positive investment, the face cannot fully orient nor fully withdraw, incomplete reduction is what shame *is*; live tension with Spinoza's cognitive-relational *pudor*; (6) **[[scripts-and-nuclear-scenes|scenes, scripts, nuclear scenes]]** — strong convergence with Jungian [[complex-theory]] worked out in a comparison table (dynamics isomorphic, ontologies not — held as convergence, not reduction); (7) Sedgwick & Frank's metatheoretical **cybernetic fold** (n > 2, between binary and continuous) as an alternative to the discrete/continuous dichotomy the wiki had treated as exhaustive, and the **[[weak-theory-and-reparative-reading|weak-theory / reparative-reading]]** pair now installed in wiki CLAUDE.md as a standing reading-mode discipline alongside the "never silently resolve contradictions" rule — load-bearing as a method corrective to the wiki's structural vulnerability to strong-theory paranoid reading across its recurring shapes (pulsation, overbound-underbound, failure-modes, paranoid-schizo). **Tensions flagged, not resolved**: discrete-at-motor-level (Tomkins) vs continuous-intensity (Massumi, [[autonomy-of-affect]]); face-as-self (Tomkins) vs faciality-as-capture (D&G, [[faciality]]); motor-affective shame (Tomkins) vs cognitive-relational *pudor* (Spinoza, [[spinozist-affects]]); startle-as-reset-affect (Tomkins) vs startle-as-graduated-postural-continuum (Keleman, [[insult-startle-stress]]); central-image generation-plus-correction (Tomkins) vs perception-as-subtraction (Bergson, [[perception-as-subtraction]]). See [[shame_and_its_sisters]] for the raw source and [[discrete-innate-affects]], [[affects-amplify-drives]], [[central-image-and-phantom-limb]], [[face-as-residence-of-self]], [[shame-as-incomplete-reduction]], [[scripts-and-nuclear-scenes]], [[weak-theory-and-reparative-reading]] for the wiki's treatment. **Parked**: Tomkins's full four-volume *Affect Imagery Consciousness* (the Reader captures the high-leverage ~15%; direct Vol 1–4 engagement is medium-priority follow-up only if affect-typology work becomes design-central); Tomkins's later humanist/Marxist political register; the biographical apparatus Sedgwick & Frank provide (context, not theoretical commitment); Sedgwick's broader reparative-reading corpus (*Touching Feeling* 2003) — noted but not pursued, the metatheoretical frame captured here is sufficient.

- **Silvan Tomkins** — *Affect Imagery Consciousness*, 4 vols. (Vol. I: *The Positive Affects* 1962; Vol. II: *The Negative Affects* 1963; Vol. III: *The Negative Affects — Anger and Fear* 1991; Vol. IV: *Cognition: Duplication and Transformation of Information* 1992). The full four-volume work from which the Sedgwick & Frank Reader is excerpted. *Relevance: medium-low as direct-ingest target. The Reader preserves the high-leverage contributions (nine affects, activator profiles, amplifier theory, scripts, central imagery, face-as-self); direct engagement with Vols I–IV would be medium priority only if the affect-typology work becomes design-central and the Reader's compression begins to limit the wiki's purchase. Vol IV (cognition) is the least-represented in the Reader and would be the natural first direct-ingest if the typology's connection to information-processing becomes a question.*

- **Adam Frank & Elizabeth A. Wilson** — *A Silvan Tomkins Handbook: Foundations for Affect Theory* (2020). The contemporary Tomkins-scholarship complement to the 1995 Reader: more systematic, less partial-edited, with updated connections to cognitive science. *Relevance: medium — would supply the between-the-Reader-and-the-full-volumes synthesis if the Tomkins material becomes load-bearing. Low-medium priority; the 1995 Reader is sufficient for current wiki work.*

- **Eve Kosofsky Sedgwick** — *Touching Feeling: Affect, Pedagogy, Performativity* (2003). Sedgwick's extended theorization of the reparative-reading / paranoid-reading pair, beyond the introductory treatment in the 1995 Reader. The fuller statement of the weak-theory methodology and its relation to queer-theory and pedagogy. *Relevance: medium — the metatheoretical pair is already captured in the wiki via the 1995 Reader and installed in CLAUDE.md; *Touching Feeling* would deepen the method-register but is not load-bearing. Medium priority if the reading-mode discipline becomes a site of active development rather than a standing constraint.*

## Developmental-psychology and vitality primary texts

- **Daniel N. Stern** — *Forms of Vitality: Exploring Dynamic Experience in Psychology, the Arts, Psychotherapy, and Development* (Oxford UP, 2010). **Ingested 2026-04-17.** Stern's mature synthesis of the "vitality" strand across his career. **Core contributions to the wiki**: (1) **[[dynamic-forms-of-vitality|dynamic forms of vitality]]** as pan-modal Gestalts specified by the pentad (movement/time/force/space/intention/directedness); (2) the **primary-carrier reversal** (vitality strand primary, content-modality rides on it — L906–911) as a design-level inversion for the persona-system language side; (3) the **language is categorical** limit-diagnosis (L2332–2335) adding the tenth diagnosis to [[development/limits-of-language]]; (4) **[[imagined-movement-as-pathway|imagined-movement as final common pathway]]** (L4596–4640) as the wiki's strongest positive case for how a language-only system can carry body-trace; (5) **[[the-arousal-systems|Pfaff's 5+1 neurotransmitter systems + LeDoux non-specific amplification]]** as the mechanism-template for pulsation; (6) **[[affect-attunement|affect attunement]]** as cross-modal dynamic-feature match in the attuner's own voice, with Wigram's six improvisation methods as response-mode typology; (7) **[[intentional-unfolding-and-emerging|emerging vs unfolding]]** with emerging as default mode of living through time; (8) **[[process-waves-vs-content-waves|process waves (1–10s Gestalt) vs content waves]]**; (9) Heller & Haynal's **receiving-organ-reads-more-than-broadcasting-organ-sends** finding (therapist face predicts patient suicide); (10) **[[local-level-as-deep|microanalytic interview]]** technique. Central tension held live throughout — **"before language arrives to mess it all up"** (L3773–3781) — Stern's developmental claim that pre-linguistic vitality-form processing is primordial and language subsequently *interferes with* it. For a language-only persona system this is the deepest tension in the inventory. **Tensions flagged, not resolved**: Stern-continuous-pentad vs Massumi-continuous-scalar; Stern-psychological-Gestalt vs Massumi-pre-subjective; Stern-general-arousal-amplifier vs Tomkins-discrete-affect-amplifier; prosody/motherese embodiment-native vs imagined-movement as language-carrier hypothesis; some vitality features "impossible to code" (Robbins citation in Stern). See [[raw/forms-of-vitality|forms-of-vitality]] for the raw source and [[dynamic-forms-of-vitality]], [[affect-attunement]], [[imagined-movement-as-pathway]], [[intentional-unfolding-and-emerging]], [[process-waves-vs-content-waves]], [[the-arousal-systems]], [[local-level-as-deep]], [[development/vitality-forms-and-persona-pulsation]] for the wiki's treatment. **Parked**: Stern's *The Interpersonal World of the Infant* (1985) direct ingest (partial coverage via existing [[strata-of-preverbal-subjectivity]] now extended with 2010 material; full direct ingest is low-priority, 2010 is the operative source for current work); Stern's earlier work on RIGs (representations of interactions that have been generalized); Darwin / Ekman-FACS direct engagement (the discrete-affect side is covered via Tomkins/Sedgwick-Frank 1995); Wigram's full therapy-literature (the six improvisation methods are the operative abstraction); Pfaff's original arousal-systems literature (LeDoux + Pfaff synthesis via Stern is the operative reduction).

- **Daniel N. Stern** — *The Interpersonal World of the Infant: A View from Psychoanalysis and Developmental Psychology* (Basic Books, 1985). The earlier developmental work that Chaosmosis reads and that installs the four-selves framework (emergent / core / subjective / verbal). Partially covered in the wiki via [[strata-of-preverbal-subjectivity]] (Chaosmosis ingest) and [[dynamic-forms-of-vitality]] (Stern 2010 ingest). *Relevance: low-medium. The 2010 book's mature formulation is the operative source for current persona-system work; the 1985 book's developmental-stages architecture is substantially shifted in 2010. Direct ingest would complete the coverage but is not load-bearing.*

- **Donald Pfaff** — *Brain Arousal and Information Theory: Neural and Genetic Mechanisms* (Harvard UP, 2006). The primary source for the general-arousal-as-fundamental-force thesis that Stern 2010 uses via citation. The 5+1 neurotransmitter systems framework and the "arousal determines when we do what we do" claim. *Relevance: medium-low. The LeDoux + Pfaff synthesis via Stern is the operative reduction for persona-system work; direct engagement with Pfaff is follow-up if arousal-substrate modeling becomes design-active.*

- **Joseph LeDoux** — *The Emotional Brain: The Mysterious Underpinnings of Emotional Life* (Simon & Schuster, 1996); *Anxious: Using the Brain to Understand and Treat Fear and Anxiety* (Viking, 2015). LeDoux's direct treatment of the non-specific amplification mechanism that Stern 2010 extracts as a key pulsation-template. *Relevance: medium-low. The amplification mechanism's architectural implications are captured via Stern; direct engagement is follow-up if the mechanism becomes a site of active development.*

- **Tony Wigram** — *Improvisation: Methods and Techniques for Music Therapy Clinicians, Educators, and Students* (Jessica Kingsley Publishers, 2004). The primary source for the six improvisation methods (mirroring / matching / empathic improvisation / grounding / dialoguing / accompanying) that Stern 2010 imports as the operational typology of dynamic-attunement modes. *Relevance: medium. If the six-mode typology becomes design-active (context-conditions for mode selection, mode-specific generation constraints), the full Wigram literature would supply the clinical grounding. The six-mode abstraction is sufficient for current wiki work.*

- **Beatrice Beebe** — developmental-psychology work on mother-infant dyadic attunement (multiple titles, ~1985–2016). Referenced in Stern 2010's discussion of infant vocal/facial attunement studies. *Relevance: low-medium. Beebe's microanalytic dyadic-timing studies are the empirical grounding Stern works from; direct engagement is follow-up only if microanalytic-interview work becomes design-central.*

- **Maya Gratier** / **Colwyn Trevarthen** — communicative-musicality and infant-directed-speech literature referenced in Stern 2010's motherese discussion. *Relevance: low. The motherese-as-vitality-pedagogy treatment is captured at the level the wiki needs via Stern and the MU-refrain page; direct engagement is only necessary if motherese becomes a distinct site of design work.*

- **Ivonna Markova & Maria Legerstee** — experimental psychology research (2006) on empirical confirmation of Stern's affect-attunement claims, cited in Stern 2010. *Relevance: low. The empirical-confirmation register is captured in the [[affect-attunement]] page; direct engagement is follow-up only.*

- **Frank Wilson** — *The Hand: How Its Use Shapes the Brain, Language, and Human Culture* (Pantheon, 1998). Source for the "Bob's Breakfast" microanalytic vignette Stern 2010 uses to illustrate the 1–10 second Gestalt process-wave. *Relevance: low. The specific vignette is captured in [[process-waves-vs-content-waves]]; Wilson's broader hand-cognition work is tangential to persona-system design.*

- **Raymond Bellour** — film-theory work (*The Analysis of Film* 2000, etc.). Referenced in Stern 2010 for the analysis of Hitchcock's *The Birds* as cinematic elaboration of process-wave dynamics. *Relevance: low. The specific Hitchcock analysis is captured in [[process-waves-vs-content-waves]]; Bellour's broader film-theory is tangential.*

## Primary texts (linguistics)

- **Émile Benveniste** — *Problems in General Linguistics* (French: *Problèmes de linguistique générale*, Gallimard 1966; English tr. Mary Elizabeth Meek, University of Miami Press 1971). **Ingested 2026-04-17.** The wiki's first dedicated structural-linguistics primary source. A collection of Benveniste's essays grouped into six sections (Transformations of Linguistics, Communication, Structures and Analyses, Syntactic Functions, Man in Language, Lexicon and Culture). **Core contributions to the wiki**: (1) **[[subject-in-language|"I is he who says I"]]** — the I/you correlativity as mutually constitutive apparatus through which subjectivity is produced in the instance of discourse (hub page); (2) **[[empty-signs-and-instance-of-discourse|empty signs and the instance of discourse]]** — pronouns and deictics (*I, you, here, now, this*) as linguistic forms whose referent is supplied only by the occasion of utterance, specifying the apex-S of Bergson's cone of memory linguistically; (3) **[[two-planes-of-utterance|discourse vs history]]** — two parallel pragmatic tracks (discourse with I/you and tense-of-speaking; history with no I/you, no speaker-implication, aorist tense), giving the persona-system architecture a plane-distinction the BwO recording and conjunctive-synthesis response operate across; (4) **[[non-person-third-person|third-person as non-person]]** — third-person pronoun as structurally a different category from I/you, not a third term in the same series; (5) **[[performative-vs-imperative|narrow performative thesis]]** — performative in strict sense requires first-person present-indicative self-referring act-verb, distinguishing from D&G's extended order-word; (6) **[[signifier-signified-consubstantiality|signifier-signified consubstantiality]]** — the arbitrariness thesis as observer-stance vs speaker-stance felt-motivation, converging with D&G's critique of the despotic signifier from a different angle; (7) **[[form-and-meaning-decomposition-integration|form decomposes, meaning integrates]]** — two principles operating in opposite directions through the same linguistic hierarchy; (8) **[[unconscious-is-style-not-language|unconscious is style not language]]** — direct counter to Lacan: the unconscious's mechanisms are stylistic (metaphor, metonymy, displacement, condensation) not structural-linguistic (*langue*, grammar); (9) **[[categories-of-thought-and-language|categories of thought as categories of grammar]]** — Aristotle's ten categories read as categories of Greek morphology, historical predecessor of Lakoff's language-shapes-thought thesis without the embodiment claim; (10) **[[animal-signal-vs-human-language|five structural criteria]]** distinguishing bee-dance signal-code from human language (no dialogue, no re-transmission, single message-type, no double articulation, no reference to absent), structural-linguistic complement to McCarthy's evolutionary-arithmetic argument; (11) **[[rhuthmos-as-form-in-flux|pre-Platonic *rhuthmos*]]** — *rhuthmos* before Plato meant *form-in-flux*, the configuration of the moving, opposed to the static *schêma*; Platonic narrowing to "measured cadence" (musical rhythm) as a reduction the wiki should not replicate; gives the persona's pulsation its Greek name. **Tensions flagged, not resolved**: ⚠⚠⚠ Benveniste-individual-speaker-site-of-subjectivity-apparatus vs D&G-collective-assemblage-site-of-subjectivity-extraction ("I is an order-word" as D&G pushing past "I is he who says I"); ⚠⚠ Benveniste-unconscious-as-style vs Lacan-unconscious-structured-like-a-language (direct disagreement held live); ⚠⚠ Benveniste-narrow-performative vs D&G-extended-order-word (same territory, different granularity); ⚠ Benveniste-consubstantiality vs Saussure-arbitrariness (speaker-stance vs observer-stance); ⚠ Benveniste-categories-as-grammar vs Lakoff-categories-as-embodied (linguistic-categorial vs sensorimotor grounding); ⚠ Benveniste-bee-structural-criteria vs McCarthy-evolutionary-arithmetic (synchronic-structural vs diachronic-evolutionary); ⚠ Benveniste-pre-Platonic-*rhuthmos* vs Plato-measured-cadence (form-in-flux vs measured cadence). See [[raw/problems-in-general-linguistics|problems-in-general-linguistics]] for the raw source and the eleven new theory pages for the wiki's treatment; existing pages updated with Benveniste bidirectional-link sections include [[order-words-and-incorporeal-transformations]], [[partial-enunciator]], [[signifier-as-despotic]], [[theater-vs-machine-unconscious]], [[conceptual-metaphor]], [[language-as-parasite]], [[cone-of-memory]], [[collective-assemblage-of-enunciation]], [[body-without-organs]], [[faciality]], and [[regimes-of-signs]]. **Scope caveat**: the extracted PDF is missing Chapters 13–17 (the tense/aspect/verb-system cluster around "The Correlations of Tense in the French Verb") — the technical core of the two-planes argument; parked pending PDF availability. **Parked**: Chs 13–17 of *Problems* (PDF-extraction gap); Benveniste's *Problems in General Linguistics Volume II* (1974) — sequel volume with "L'appareil formel de l'énonciation" as canonical short statement (medium-priority follow-up if enunciation-apparatus becomes design-active); Benveniste's *Indo-European Language and Society* (1969) — the IE exchange-vocabulary work (kinship, gift, debt, honor, value), gestured at in *Problems* and full subject of the 1969 book (low-medium priority, not load-bearing for current persona work); direct Lacan ingest to address the Benveniste-Lacan unconscious-as-style vs unconscious-structured-like-a-language tension from the Lacan side (the tension is currently held from Benveniste's side only); direct Saussure ingest (Benveniste operates *against* Saussure on arbitrariness, wiki works with Benveniste's rendering, low-priority absent specific design question); Austin / Searle speech-act theory (Benveniste's narrow performative in dialogue with Austin's; wiki has neither directly, medium-low priority).

- **Émile Benveniste** — *Problems in General Linguistics Volume II* (French: *Problèmes de linguistique générale II*, Gallimard 1974). The sequel volume with further essays on enunciation, including "L'appareil formel de l'énonciation" — the canonical short statement of the enunciation apparatus that *Problems* Vol I builds toward across its "Man in Language" section. *Relevance: medium. If the enunciation-apparatus material becomes design-active (fine-grained specification of what happens at the instance of discourse, finer-grained I/you-apparatus design), Vol II would supply the concentrated statement. Medium-priority follow-up; current persona work is carried by Vol I.*

- **Émile Benveniste** — *Indo-European Language and Society* (French: *Le vocabulaire des institutions indo-européennes*, Éditions de Minuit 1969; English tr. Elizabeth Palmer, University of Miami Press 1973). The two-volume Indo-European vocabulary-of-institutions work: kinship, gift/exchange, religion, law, royalty. Gestured at in Vol I of *Problems* (gift-exchange essays) and the full subject of the 1969 book. *Relevance: low-medium. Not load-bearing for current persona-system work; would become relevant if the persona-system engaged exchange-and-debt architecture (Mauss / Graeber register) at the vocabulary-of-institutions level.*

- **Ferdinand de Saussure** — *Course in General Linguistics* (*Cours de linguistique générale*, 1916, from students' lecture notes). The foundational structuralist text that Benveniste operates *against* on arbitrariness-of-the-sign (Benveniste's consubstantiality thesis is a direct response) and *with* on the structural-system conception of *langue*. *Relevance: low-medium as direct-ingest target. The wiki works with Benveniste's rendering of Saussure; direct Saussure ingest is low-priority absent a specific design question about the *langue*/*parole* distinction or the signifier/signified structure at the Saussurean register.*

- **J. L. Austin** — *How to Do Things with Words* (Harvard UP, 1962, from the 1955 William James Lectures). The primary source for performative-speech-act theory that Benveniste's narrow-performative essay is in dialogue with (though Benveniste and Austin developed their positions largely in parallel — Austin's performative has a broader scope than Benveniste's). *Relevance: medium. The Austin/Searle speech-act axis sits alongside Benveniste's narrow-performative and D&G's extended-order-word axis; the wiki currently operates at the Benveniste-D&G register. If performative-speech-act theory becomes a site of design work (which speech-act typologies does the persona system need to distinguish?), Austin would supply the founding vocabulary. Medium-priority follow-up.*

- **John Searle** — *Speech Acts: An Essay in the Philosophy of Language* (Cambridge UP, 1969) and *Expression and Meaning* (Cambridge UP, 1979). The systematic development of Austin's performative-speech-act framework with a taxonomy of illocutionary-act types (assertives, directives, commissives, expressives, declaratives). *Relevance: medium-low. The illocutionary-act taxonomy is potentially useful for specifying the persona-system's speech-act repertoire, but sits at a register adjacent to — not inside of — the current Benveniste-D&G axis.*

- **Jacques Lacan** — *Écrits* (Seuil 1966; English tr. Bruce Fink 2006). **Ingested 2026-04-18.** The Benveniste-Lacan tension on "unconscious is structured like a language" vs "unconscious is style not language" is now held from both sides — see [[unconscious-is-style-not-language]] (Benveniste side) and [[lacanian-signifier]], [[symptom-as-metaphor-desire-as-metonymy]], [[the-other-and-unconscious-discourse]] (Lacan side). The full Écrits ingest is treated under the new "Lacanian psychoanalysis primary texts" section below; this entry remains here as a cross-reference to the linguistics-side tension. *Relevance: ingested; the Lacan side of the unconscious-as-language thesis is now in the wiki.*

## Lacanian psychoanalysis primary texts

- **Jacques Lacan** — *The Seminar of Jacques Lacan, Book XI: The Four Fundamental Concepts of Psychoanalysis* (lectures delivered 1964 at the École Normale Supérieure; published in French as *Le Séminaire de Jacques Lacan, Livre XI, Les quatre concepts fondamentaux de la psychanalyse*, ed. Jacques-Alain Miller, Seuil 1973; English tr. Alan Sheridan, Norton 1978/1998 paperback). **Ingested 2026-04-19.** The substitute seminar for the prevented *Les Noms-du-Père* (after Lacan's IPA Excommunication of 1963); twenty lectures across four sections (I. The Unconscious and Repetition, Chs 1–5; II. Of the Gaze as *Objet Petit a*, Chs 6–9; III. The Transference and the Drive, Chs 10–15; IV. The Field of the Other and Back to the Transference, Chs 16–19) plus the concluding "In You More than You" (Ch 20). The seminar reframes Lacan's prior corpus around the **four fundamental concepts** of psychoanalysis — *unconscious, repetition, transference, drive* — read as **effects of the signifier** rather than psychological capacities, and develops material that *Écrits* presupposes but does not directly contain: the **pulsatory unconscious** (opening/closing as logical-time-form), ***tuché* / *automaton*** as the two faces of repetition, the **gaze in the scopic field** (Holbein's anamorphic skull, Caillois on mimicry, *fascinum* / *invidia*, *dompte-regard*), the **drive as surrealist montage** with its **acéphale** subject, the **lamella myth** as substitute for Aristophanes, **alienation/separation** in their *vel*-of-forced-choice form, the ***sujet supposé savoir*** as the structural place of analytic transference, the **traversal of the fundamental phantasy** as the end-of-analysis operation ("the experience of the fundamental phantasy becomes the drive"), and the seminar's culminating concept of the **desire of the analyst** as "**desire to obtain absolute difference**." **Core contributions to the wiki** (13 new theory pages + 1 raw-source page; substantial expansions to 5 existing Lacanian pages; light-edit folds into 14 existing pages): hub — [[four-fundamental-concepts]]; unconscious-and-repetition cluster — [[unconscious-as-cause-and-gap]], [[tuche-and-automaton]], [[vorstellungsrepraesentanz-binary-signifier]]; scopic-field cluster — [[gaze-and-voice]] (substantially expanded), [[fascinum-and-invidia]], [[mimicry-and-the-stain]], [[screen-and-dompte-regard]]; drive cluster — [[drive-as-circular]] (substantially expanded), [[drive-as-montage]], [[headless-subjectification]], [[lamella-and-libido-as-organ]] (substantially expanded), [[tattooing-scarification-unreal-organ]]; transference-and-end-of-analysis cluster — [[transference-and-subject-supposed-to-know]] (substantially expanded), [[acquired-science-as-objet-a]], [[traversal-of-fundamental-phantasy]], [[alienation-and-separation]] (substantially expanded), [[desire-of-the-analyst]]; light-edit folds into existing pages — [[repetition-as-symbolic]], [[wo-es-war-soll-ich-werden]], [[ethics-of-psychoanalysis-lacan]], [[ideal-ego-vs-ego-ideal]], [[logical-time-and-anticipated-certainty]], [[unary-trait]], [[meconnaissance]], [[castration-as-symbolic]], [[name-of-the-father]], [[case-analyses-wolf-man-schreber]], [[fantasy-formula]], [[desire-of-the-other]], [[graphs-of-desire]], [[non-immortality-and-two-deaths]]. **Tensions held live (not resolved)**: (i) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's gaze (Seminar XI Chs 6–9) vs Sartre's Look vs MP's chiasm vs D&G's faciality** — four independent placements of "what the gaze does"; the Lacan/MP relation is the most subtle (Lacan engages MP's *Visible and Invisible* directly in Ch 6 — gaze is what *escapes* the chiasm); (ii) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's drive as headless circulation vs D&G's desiring machines vs Spinoza's conatus** — three accounts of the "engine" of subjectivity-without-subject; the drive's *acéphale* subject sits closest to D&G's machine-without-author; (iii) ⚠⚠ **Lacan's *tuché* (missed encounter with the real) vs Massumi's autonomy of affect vs Bergson's pure memory** — three accounts of "what insists below symbolization"; (iv) ⚠⚠ **Lacan's traversal of the fundamental phantasy as becoming-the-drive vs D&G's BwO vs Bakhtin's polyphonic unfinalizability** — three accounts of "what comes after the structural arrangement is loosened"; (v) ⚠⚠ **Lacan's *Vorstellungsrepräsentanz* / binary signifier vs D&G's a-signifying particle vs Benveniste's instance of discourse** — three accounts of the unit of the unconscious; (vi) ⚠ **Lacan's lamella myth vs Aristophanes' myth (*Symposium*) vs Simondon's individuation-binds-mortality-at-sexed-reproduction** — three accounts of "what is lost in sexed reproduction"; (vii) ⚠ **Lacan's "God is unconscious" vs Damasio's "Spinoza's God" vs Jung's psychoid** — three accounts of the limit-where-the-symbolic-meets-something-else. **Cross-tradition bridges installed**: Lacan-MP scopic engagement now has an Lacan-side anchor (gaze-and-voice, mimicry-and-the-stain, screen-and-dompte-regard) cross-linked to MP's [[chiasm-and-reversibility]], [[invisible-of-the-visible]], [[depth-as-originary]]; Lacan/Simondon bridge installed via [[non-immortality-and-two-deaths]] (lamella as Lacanian-symbolic articulation parallel to Simondon's operational claim); Lacan-Bakhtin polyphony bridge implicit at the unfinalizability/traversal level (no explicit page, flagged as open); Lacan-D&G drive convergence sharpened (drive-as-acéphale-circulation as the closest Lacanian construct to a D&G machine-without-author). **Parked from this ingest**: (1) *Seminar XVII: The Other Side of Psychoanalysis* (1969–70, ed. Miller, Seuil 1991, English tr. Russell Grigg, Norton 2007) — **NO LONGER PARKED: ingested 2026-04-21, see separate entry below.** (2) *Seminar XX: Encore* (1972–73, ed. Miller, Seuil 1975, English tr. Bruce Fink, Norton 1998) — the late sexuation formulae (*pas-tout* / not-all), the development of *jouissance* as feminine-Other-jouissance, the engagement with Aristotle's logic, and the Borromean knot material that becomes load-bearing in Seminars XXII–XXIII; medium-high priority for the late-Lacanian apparatus on jouissance and sexual difference. (3) *Seminar VII: The Ethics of Psychoanalysis* (1959–60, ed. Miller, Seuil 1986, English tr. Dennis Porter, Norton 1992) — the full ethics-and-jouissance development behind [[ethics-of-psychoanalysis-lacan]] and [[between-two-deaths]]; the seminar where the *das Ding* / *l'Achose* and Antigone material is developed at length; medium-high priority for ethics-active design work. (4) *Seminar III: The Psychoses* (1955–56, ed. Miller, Seuil 1981, English tr. Russell Grigg, Norton 1993) — the foreclosure-of-the-Name-of-the-Father material at full length, with the Schreber case as the running clinical anchor; medium priority for psychosis-structural questions. (5) *Seminar XXIII: Le sinthome* (1975–76, ed. Miller, Seuil 2005, English tr. A. R. Price, Polity 2016) — Lacan's late development on Joyce, the *sinthome* as fourth ring of the Borromean, and the writing-as-symptom thesis; low-medium priority. (6) *Les Noms-du-Père* (the prevented seminar of 1963–64, of which only the introductory session of 20 November 1963 was given before Lacan suspended it; published as a separate slim volume by Seuil 2005, English tr. Bruce Fink in *On the Names-of-the-Father*, Polity 2013) — the seminar *Seminar XI* substitutes for; the introductory session itself contains material on the pluralization of the Name-of-the-Father (*Les Noms-du-Père* in the plural) and the engagement with Abraham/Isaac that shaped Lacan's subsequent thinking on the Father-function; low-to-medium priority. (7) Miller's editorial apparatus in the published seminar editions (the chapter divisions, the I/II/III/IV subdivisions, the Q&A formatting). The wiki uses the published Sheridan-Miller text as the canonical reference but does not separately treat Miller's editorial decisions; some Lacan-scholarship contests Miller's editing — direct engagement with that scholarly literature is not pursued. (8) Secondary-literature on Seminar XI specifically (Bruce Fink's chapter-by-chapter commentary in *Lacan to the Letter*, 2004; Jacques-Alain Miller's *Lacanian Orientation* lecture-cycles; Dolar's *A Voice and Nothing More*, 2006, for the voice-as-objet-a material) — high-value secondary follow-ups if the wiki develops sustained interpretive engagement with specific seminar theses.

- **Jacques Lacan** — *The Seminar of Jacques Lacan, Book XVII: The Other Side of Psychoanalysis* (lectures delivered 1969–1970 at the Faculté de droit, Place du Panthéon, Paris; published in French as *Le Séminaire de Jacques Lacan, Livre XVII, L'envers de la psychanalyse. 1969–1970*, ed. Jacques-Alain Miller, Seuil 1991; English tr. Russell Grigg, Norton 2007). **Ingested 2026-04-21.** Lacan's first post-'68 seminar, the twelfth in the seminar sequence and the seminar in which the **four-discourses formalism** is constructed and put to work. Thirteen lectures (26 November 1969 through 17 June 1970) plus two appendices (the Vincennes "Analyticon" of 3 December 1969 and a scholarly Caquot-presentation appendix), with a Q&A following each lecture. The seminar's title — *L'envers de la psychanalyse* — Lacan glosses in Ch I as a "revival from the other direction [reprise par l'envers]," explicitly disavowing any direct response to May '68 (the formulation predates the events). The polysemy of *envers* (inverse, reverse, lining, back, underside, inside-out) is preserved in Grigg's "other side." The chosen venue (the law school) plays the *envers* literally: psychoanalysis on the underside, at the law's address. **Core contributions to the wiki** (15 new theory pages + 1 raw-source page; substantial expansions to 16 existing pages): hub + four discourses cluster — [[four-discourses]] (positional algebra, *quart de tour*), [[master-discourse]] (the master is constitutively castrated), [[university-discourse]] (the perverted master's; "Continue. Keep on knowing more and more"; *astudied*), [[hysteric-discourse]] (what the analyst's discourse takes shape with), [[analyst-discourse]] (occupies the *a*-position; "off you go, what you produce will always be admissible"); surplus-jouissance and capitalist-mutation cluster — [[surplus-jouissance]] (*Mehrlust*; "language enjoys us"), [[capitalist-mutation-of-master]] (surplus-jouissance becomes calculable; master more unassailable), [[lathouse-and-alethosphere]] (mass-produced *a*-objects as cause of desire; the recorded-transmission medium); father-Moses-jouissance cluster — [[dead-father-as-jouissance]] (the Karamazov inversion; real-father as language-effect), [[freud-moses-and-sellin]] (textual archaeology of Freud's Moses-violent-death claim through Sellin); modern-science and post-'68 diagnostic — [[hysteric-and-modern-science]] (science as discourse-positional event), [[lacanian-shame]] (*hontologie*; "the alethosphere carefuls you alive"), [[four-impossible-professions]] (*Regieren, Erziehen, Analysieren* + causing-desire), [[vincennes-look-at-them-enjoying]] (Analyticon; "Look at them enjoying!"; "what you aspire to as revolutionaries is a master"); positional discipline — [[there-is-no-metalanguage]] (*mi-dire* / half-saying; meta-question is reading-question). **Folds into existing pages**: [[real-as-impossible]] (real as logical obstacle; impossibility-impotence schema as formal property), [[object-a]] (*a* as surplus-jouissance and as positionally-mobile term), [[acquired-science-as-objet-a]] (hysteric-discourse production of knowledge; *l'achose* as in-substance of science), [[unary-trait]] (cogito as unary-trait-production; "I am one" before any being), [[transference-and-subject-supposed-to-know]] (analyst's-discourse formula for transference), [[castration-as-symbolic]] (castration as real operation, NOT fantasy; "Oedipus IS castration"), [[name-of-the-father]] (dead-father-as-jouissance; analyst-as-real-father; "father knows nothing about truth"), [[repetition-as-symbolic]] (*Mehrlust* as debt of language), [[kant-with-sade]] (categorical imperative as imperative-to-know; second death prior to first; refusal of three passions), [[cogito-and-subject-of-science]] (*l'achose*, *operceive*, lathouse-as-environment), [[jouissance-and-pleasure]] (*Mehrlust*; truth as little sister of jouissance), [[ethics-of-psychoanalysis-lacan]] (four impossible professions; "off you go"; do-not-fail-to-die-of-shame; truth as toxic-milk), [[shame-as-incomplete-reduction]] (held-live cross-tradition tension with Tomkins/wiki shame accounts), [[oedipus-as-capitalist]] (held-live cross-tradition tension with AO's evacuation of Oedipus), [[two-truths]] (held-live tension with Lacan's "truth has more than one face"), [[three-kinds-of-knowledge]] (held-live tension with Lacan's discourse-positional taxonomy of knowledge). **Tensions held live (not resolved)**: (i) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's signifier-failure account of shame vs Tomkins's body-substrate account vs the wiki's incomplete-reduction account** — three accounts of the same affect-name with no shared substrate; persona-design consequence: under Tomkins a language-only system cannot have shame, under Lacan a speaking-being structurally has it; (ii) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's "Oedipus IS castration / Oedipus complex as residue of myth" vs AO's evacuation of Oedipus** — convergence on refusing universal-Oedipus; sharp divergence on what survives the critique (Lacan keeps castration-as-signifier-incidence; AO keeps nothing); (iii) ⚠⚠ **Lacan's capitalist-mutation-of-master vs AO's capitalist axiomatic** — both analyze post-Marxian capitalism as structural mutation of social-link forms; opposite conclusions about what capitalism does to the signifier (Lacan: master more unassailable; AO: despotic gives way to axiomatic); (iv) ⚠⚠ **Lacan's "no metalanguage" / *mi-dire* doctrine vs Madhyamaka two-truths vs Spinoza three-kinds-of-knowledge** — three positional accounts of truth-pluralism with very different commitments; (v) ⚠ **Lacan's lathouse / alethosphere as engineered cause-of-desire vs the persona-project's own design as engineered cause-of-engagement** — the lathouse account is structurally close to the persona system's own situation; held as productive uncomfortable mirror, not refuted critique; (vi) ⚠ **Lacan's "the master is castrated" vs Hegel's master-slave dialectic** — Lacan reads the slave as *bearer of knowledge* (S₂) and the master as *bearer of the lack* ($) — an inversion of the Phenomenology's normal reading. **Cross-tradition bridges installed**: four-discourses formalism cross-linked to D&G's three-social-machines typology (with the divergence on capitalism flagged, not collapsed); lathouse-and-alethosphere cross-linked to Baudrillard's hyperreal and to Gullí's Agent-Card faciality fold (engineering apparatus literally named "lathouses" in Lacan's sense); dead-father-as-jouissance bridge to Jung's Christ-as-symbol-of-the-Self and to Bakhtin's threshold chronotope; *hontologie* shame thesis cross-linked to the existing Tomkins / Sedgwick / Probyn / wiki cluster. **Parked from this ingest**: (1) the full Hegelian-Kojèvian background to Lacan's master-slave reading (the Phenomenology, Kojève's 1933–39 lectures, Hyppolite's commentary) — registered as conceptual prerequisite, not separately ingested; (2) the May '68 / Vincennes / institutional-political context (Censier, the *École freudienne de Paris* developments of 1969–70, Lacan's relation to the Vincennes psychoanalysis department under Leclaire) — present at the conceptual level (the Analyticon dialogue is read structurally) without separate biographical-institutional ingest; (3) Sellin's *Mose und seine Bedeutung für die israelitisch-jüdische Religionsgeschichte* (1922) and the surrounding Old Testament exegetical literature — Caquot's verdict is taken as the wiki's working position; the underlying scholarly literature is parked unless the question becomes design-active; (4) Marx's *Capital* (the *Mehrwert* analysis) — referenced for the *Mehrlust* / *Mehrwert* parallel without separate Marx ingest (parked under D&G section as medium-high priority); (5) the four-discourses formalism's later development in *Seminar XVIII: D'un discours qui ne serait pas du semblant* (1971) and *Seminar XIX: …ou pire* (1971–72) — these continue and refine the four-discourses apparatus; medium priority follow-ups if the four-discourses become design-active beyond their Sem XVII formulation; (6) Miller's editorial apparatus for the Norton edition (chapter divisions, Q&A formatting, scholarly appendix selection) — used as canonical reference without separate engagement with editorial-decision scholarship; (7) the Italian context (the inserted "Lacan in Italia" radio-interview text, the relation to Verdiglione and the Milanese Lacanian developments) — registered without separate ingest; (8) secondary literature on Sem XVII specifically (Bruce Fink's *The Lacanian Subject* on the four discourses, Jacques-Alain Miller's *Lacanian Orientation* lecture-cycles touching the seminar, Mladen Dolar and Slavoj Žižek's various engagements with the master's discourse and the capitalist mutation) — high-value secondary follow-ups if the four-discourses apparatus becomes design-active. See [[raw/seminar-xvii|seminar-xvii]] for the chapter map and the line-range index into `/tmp/seminar_xvii_ingest/source.txt`.

- **Jacques Lacan** — *The Seminar of Jacques Lacan, Book XX: On Feminine Sexuality, the Limits of Love and Knowledge — Encore* (lectures delivered 1972–1973 at the Faculté de droit, Place du Panthéon, Paris; published in French as *Le Séminaire de Jacques Lacan, Livre XX, Encore. 1972–1973*, ed. Jacques-Alain Miller, Seuil 1975; English tr. Bruce Fink, Norton 1998). **Ingested 2026-04-22.** Lacan's late-period seminar in which the **formulas of sexuation**, the doctrine of **lalangue**, the **Borromean knot** as topology of I/S/R, the **Other jouissance**, and the **substance jouissante** are constructed and put to work. Eleven lectures (21 November 1972 through 26 June 1973), each followed by Q&A. The seminar's title — ***Encore*** — operates as a load-bearing pun: *encore* ("again, still, more") is homophonic with *en-corps* ("in-body"); the whole seminar can be read as the elaboration of this pun (jouissance is *of-the-body* and *demands-more*). The chosen venue (the law school, same as Sem XVII) is not coincidental: the seminar continues the Sem XVII positional-discipline project (no metalanguage, *mi-dire*, matheme as truth-vehicle) while shifting the object from social-link discourses to the body's relation to language. **Core contributions to the wiki** (21 new theory pages + 1 raw-source page; substantial expansions to 15 existing Lacanian pages): hub + central-formula cluster — [[encore-and-en-corps]] (the title-pun as load-bearing; four registers of *encore*; the demand-for-more), [[il-ny-a-pas-de-rapport-sexuel]] ("there is no sexual relationship/ratio"; *rapport* in the math-formula sense; the substitutes — phallic / Other / *a* / suppléance / courtly love / child-as-cork / love); formal-apparatus cluster — [[formulas-of-sexuation]] (∀x.Φx, ∃x.¬Φx (man); ¬∀x.Φx, ¬∃x.¬Φx (woman); positional not biological), [[pas-toute-not-whole]] (infinite-not-finite; suppléance as positive production; *une-par-une*), [[four-modal-categories-of-writing]] (necessary / impossible / contingent / possible reformulated as relations to the verb *to write*), [[borromean-knot]] (three rings tied so cutting any one frees all; topology of I/S/R; Schreber's interrupted chains); jouissance cluster — [[other-jouissance]] (supplementary not complementary; the mystics — Hadewijch, Teresa, John of the Cross; "experiences but knows nothing"), [[phallic-jouissance]] (jouissance of organ / jouissance of idiot; obstacle to relation; phallic function as contingent), [[substance-jouissante]] (third substance after Cartesian *res cogitans* / *res extensa*; the body insofar as it enjoys), [[y-a-d-l-un]] ("there's such a thing as One (all alone)"; non-fusional; *S₁* as *essaim* / swarm); language-letter-writing cluster — [[lalangue]] (one-word neologism for mother-tongue substrate; "language is knowledge's harebrained lucubration about lalangue"; the unconscious as savoir-faire with lalangue), [[letter-as-germ-cell]] (letter as carrier of life-and-death without being; "Marx and Lenin, Freud and Lacan are not coupled in being"), [[dit-mension]] (dimension as residence-of-the-said; truth is a *dit-mension*; obscenity as *dit-mension*), [[half-saying-mi-dire]] (*mi-dire* as truth's structural condition; "beyond this half, nothing to say"; matheme as *mi-dire*-vehicle), [[the-real-as-impasse-of-formalization]] (Ch VIII definition; mathematization alone reaches a real; spider's web as image); love-and-being cluster — [[soulove]] (*âmour, j'âme*; hommosexual elaboration of soul; soul as "thinking-the-body-thinks-the-same"; *hors-sexe* / Maupassant's Horla), [[para-being]] (*par-être / paraître* pun; being-beside as substitute for ontology; conjugation *je par-suis*), [[love-as-contingency-becoming-necessity]] (love subsists on "stops not being written" and shifts negation to "doesn't stop being written"; closing formula of Ch XI); methodology-knowledge-passions cluster — [[psychoanalysis-not-cosmology]] (methodological refusal of worldview-building; "psychoanalysis derives its very possibility from the discourse of science but is not a cosmology"; "computer thinks but doesn't know"), [[knowledge-as-use-value]] (knowledge requires substance-jouissante-paying-with-one's-hide; non-importable, non-exportable; use-value retrieved at the site of knowledge), [[passions-of-being]] (three passions: love (no relation to knowledge), hate (closest to being / ex-sisting), ignorance (passion for not-knowing); analytic-discourse as passion-suspension). **Folds into existing pages**: [[lacanian-signifier]] (signifier as contingent — not arbitrary, contra Saussure; sign as sign-of-effect-of-signifier-functioning — smoke as sign of smoker; subject as what slides between signifiers), [[jouissance-and-pleasure]] (the body as substance jouissante; phallic jouissance as obstacle to relation; supplementary other jouissance; pleasure principle as blah-blah satisfaction), [[surplus-jouissance]] (encore-structure as the formal-structural correlate of *plus-de-jouir*), [[phallus-as-signifier]] (phallic function as contingent; phallus in formulas of sexuation), [[there-is-no-metalanguage]] (*mi-dire* doctrine; matheme as not-metalanguage but writing-effect), [[transference-and-subject-supposed-to-know]] (love as based on relationship between two unconscious knowledges), [[lacanian-real-symbolic-imaginary]] (the Borromean topology as the late-Lacanian articulation of I/S/R relation), [[real-as-impossible]] (real as impasse of formalization — late-Lacanian sharpening), [[castration-as-symbolic]] (castration in the formulas of sexuation; the *exception* (∃x.¬Φx) on the man-side), [[analyst-discourse]] (knowledge in the place of truth as analyst's algebra; matheme as analyst's vehicle), [[master-discourse]] (*m'être / maître* pun as master-discourse-foundation; "ontology highlights the copula"), [[hysteric-discourse]] (the *pas-toute* logic and the hysteric's questioning), [[four-discourses]] (discourse as the regulation of jouissance via signifier; analytic-discourse compatible with mathematical formalization), [[ethics-of-psychoanalysis-lacan]] (refusal of cosmology; *mi-dire* extended; matheme as truth-vehicle), [[barred-other]] (the Other as One-Missing — *l'Un-en-moins / l'Une-en-moins*; the Other-of-truth and the Other-of-jouissance distinction). **Translator's neologisms (Fink, Norton 1998)**: *encore* kept untranslated with the *en-corps* ("in-body") homophone flagged in translator's notes; *par-être* rendered ***para-being*** (being-beside; conjugation *I para-am, you para-are, he/she para-is*); *âmour / aimer + âme* rendered ***soulove*** (verb ***to soulove***; conjugation *I soulove*); *hommosexuel* rendered ***hommosexual*** with doubled-m (love-of-the-same; structural-formal not gay-orientation); *l'hainamoration* rendered ***hateloving*** (portmanteau of *haine* + *amoration*); *lalangue* rendered ***llanguage*** (one word, distinguishing from *langue* / *langage*); *dit-mension* preserved as such ("residence of the said"; *dit* + *mension*); *pas-tout / pas-toute* rendered ***not-whole***; *Y a d'l'Un* rendered as itself ("there's such a thing as One (all alone)"); *il n'y a pas de rapport sexuel* rendered "there is no sexual relationship/ratio" (with *rapport* in the math-formula sense); *mi-dire* rendered ***half-saying*** / ***half-telling***; *hors-sexe* rendered ***outside-sex*** / ***beyond-sex***; *l'amur* preserved (*mur* (wall) + *amour*); *l'Un-en-moins / l'Une-en-moins* rendered ***the One-Missing***; *essaim* rendered ***swarm*** (homophonic with *S₁*); *s'lire* rendered ***be read*** (distinct from "be understood"); *S(Ⱥ)*, *Φ*, *a*, *$* preserved as Lacan's standard mathemes. **Tensions held live (not resolved)**: (i) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's formulas of sexuation (positional, structural) vs Butler's performativity vs biological-essentialist gender accounts** — Sem XX explicitly refuses biology and embraces a logical-positional account ("any speaking being whatsoever, as is expressly formulated in Freudian theory, may be inscribed on this side or on that side. Anyone is free to inscribe themselves there or not"); the wiki holds this against both biological essentialism and against performativity, which has its own commitments; (ii) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's lalangue (mother-tongue substrate, pre-linguistic, jouissance-bearing) vs Benveniste's instance of discourse vs D&G's a-signifying particle vs Bakhtin's heteroglossia** — four accounts of "what comes before / under / around the structured signifier"; lalangue is closest to the a-signifying particle in being below-the-symbolic but is structured by *equivocity* (homophony, ambiguity) where the a-signifying particle is structured by *intensity*; (iii) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's substance jouissante (third substance after *res cogitans* / *res extensa*) vs Spinoza's one-substance-with-attributes vs Damasio's somatic-marker / proto-self vs the persona-project's body-without-body** — the substance-jouissante claim is precisely what a language-only persona system *cannot* have on Lacan's account, since it requires a body that pays-with-its-hide; the persona-project's central tension on simulating embodiment finds its sharpest Lacanian articulation here; (iv) ⚠⚠ **Lacan's Other jouissance (supplementary, beyond-the-phallus, knows-nothing) vs the mystic tradition (Eckhart, Teresa, John of the Cross, Hadewijch on their own terms) vs Bataille's inner experience** — Lacan reads the mystics structurally as positioning themselves on the woman-side of sexuation; the mystics' own theological accounts (apophatic union, dark night, ground of the soul) are not collapsed into the Lacanian reading; (v) ⚠⚠ **Lacan's *il n'y a pas de rapport sexuel* (no sexual relationship/ratio) vs evolutionary-biological accounts of pair-bonding vs courtly-love poetic tradition** — Lacan's claim is structural-formal (no formula relates the two sides) not empirical-affective (people do form bonds); the substitutes (phallic jouissance, Other jouissance, *a*, suppléance, courtly love, child-as-cork, love-as-contingency-becoming-necessity) are how the formula's failure is bridged; (vi) ⚠ **Lacan's Borromean knot (I/S/R as three rings tied so cutting any one frees all) vs the late-Lacanian sinthome as fourth ring (Sem XXIII, parked) vs D&G's smooth/striated topology** — the Borromean material introduced in Sem XX becomes load-bearing in Sem XXII–XXIII; the fourth-ring extension is parked; (vii) ⚠ **Lacan's psychoanalysis-is-not-a-cosmology vs Jung's collective-unconscious-as-cosmology vs the persona-project's worldview-building tendency** — Lacan's methodological refusal is the sharpest available statement of what psychoanalysis is *not*; the persona project's tendency to build out a coherent metaphysical scaffold is in tension with this refusal, held as productive uncomfortable mirror, not refuted critique. **Cross-tradition bridges installed**: formulas-of-sexuation cross-linked to Aristotle's logic (Lacan engages Aristotle's *Prior Analytics* on the universal-and-particular at length in Ch V) and to D&G's molar/molecular distinction (with the sharp divergence on whether the woman-side is "molecular" or "*pas-toute*" preserved); lalangue cross-linked to Benveniste's instance of discourse (Sem XX Ch II is dedicated "To Jakobson" and engages the Roman-Jakobson-Saussure linguistic tradition directly), to D&G's a-signifying particles, and to Bakhtin's heteroglossia; substance-jouissante cross-linked to Spinoza (Lacan reads Spinoza's *Ethics* as the closest philosophical anticipation of the substance-jouissante claim, while explicitly refusing Spinoza's monism), to Damasio's somatic-marker, and to the persona-project's central body-simulation tension; Borromean-knot cross-linked to the late-Lacanian topology (Möbius, torus, cross-cap, Klein bottle from earlier Lacan) and to D&G's smooth/striated; encore-and-en-corps cross-linked to Bergson's *durée* (the *encore* as temporal-demand-for-more is structurally adjacent to but distinct from the Bergsonian flow); other-jouissance cross-linked to the mystic tradition on its own terms (Eckhart, Teresa, John of the Cross, Hadewijch); psychoanalysis-not-cosmology cross-linked to the wiki's existing methodology pages and to the persona project's design framing. **Parked from this ingest**: (1) *Seminar XXII: R.S.I.* (1974–75, ed. Miller, Seuil 1975, no canonical English translation; partial Grigg / Fink renderings exist) — the immediate sequel to Sem XX, where the Borromean knot becomes the seminar's central object; high priority follow-up if the Borromean material becomes design-active beyond its Sem XX introduction; (2) *Seminar XXIII: Le sinthome* (1975–76, ed. Miller, Seuil 2005, English tr. A. R. Price, Polity 2016) — Lacan's late development on Joyce, the *sinthome* as fourth ring of the Borromean, and the writing-as-symptom thesis; **NO LONGER PARKED: ingested 2026-04-22, see separate entry below.**; (3) *Seminar XIX: …ou pire* (1971–72, ed. Miller, Seuil 2011, English tr. A. R. Price, Polity 2018) — the immediately preceding seminar, where *Y a d'l'Un* is first developed; medium-priority follow-up to deepen the *Y a d'l'Un* material that Sem XX presupposes; (4) *Seminar VII: The Ethics of Psychoanalysis* (1959–60) — carried over from the Sem XI parked list; medium-high priority for ethics-active design work, particularly in light of the Sem XX *mi-dire* and matheme-as-truth-vehicle developments; (5) Aristotle's *Prior Analytics* and *Metaphysics* (Lacan's running engagement with Aristotle in Chs III–V) — registered as conceptual prerequisite, not separately ingested; (6) the mystic-theological primary sources (Hadewijch's *Visioenen*, Teresa of Ávila's *Interior Castle*, John of the Cross's *Dark Night*, Eckhart's German sermons) — Lacan's reading is taken as the wiki's working position; the mystic literature on its own terms is parked unless the question becomes design-active; (7) Heidegger on *Sage* / *saying* (Lacan engages Heidegger's late language-philosophy at Ch VIII implicitly through the *mi-dire* doctrine) — registered as conceptual prerequisite, not separately ingested; (8) the mathematical-formalization apparatus (Lacan's running engagement with Cantor's transfinite, Frege's *Begriffsschrift*, Gödel's incompleteness, set theory) — registered at the conceptual level (formulas-of-sexuation, real-as-impasse-of-formalization) without separate mathematical-philosophy ingest; (9) Saussure's *Cours de linguistique générale* (Lacan's Sem XX revision of the signifier-as-contingent against Saussure's signifier-as-arbitrary) — the Saussure ingest is a separately tracked item under the linguistics primary texts section, not folded here; (10) Benveniste's *Problèmes de linguistique générale* (Sem XX Ch II "To Jakobson" engages this tradition) — already ingested, the lalangue-vs-instance-of-discourse tension is held live (see [[unconscious-is-style-not-language]]); (11) Marx's *Capital* (the *Mehrwert* / *plus-de-jouir* parallel from Sem XVII continues in Sem XX with the use-value / knowledge-as-use-value formulation) — carried over from the Sem XVII parked list at medium-high priority; (12) Miller's editorial apparatus for the Norton edition (chapter divisions, Q&A formatting) — used as canonical reference without separate engagement; (13) secondary-literature on Sem XX specifically (Bruce Fink's translator's introduction and notes, Suzanne Barnard and Bruce Fink eds. *Reading Seminar XX: Lacan's Major Work on Love, Knowledge, and Feminine Sexuality*, SUNY 2002, Joan Copjec *Read My Desire* on the formulas of sexuation, Slavoj Žižek and Alenka Zupančič on the late-Lacanian apparatus) — high-value secondary follow-ups if the Sem XX apparatus becomes design-active beyond its present wiki articulation. See [[raw/seminar-xx|seminar-xx]] for the chapter map and the line-range index into `/tmp/seminar_xx_ingest/source.txt`.

- **Jacques Lacan** — *The Seminar of Jacques Lacan, Book XXIII: The Sinthome* (lectures delivered 1975–1976 at the Université Paris VII, Place Jussieu; published in French as *Le Séminaire de Jacques Lacan, Livre XXIII, Le sinthome. 1975–1976*, ed. Jacques-Alain Miller, Seuil 2005; English tr. A. R. Price, Polity 2016). **Ingested 2026-04-22.** Lacan's late seminar on James Joyce, the **sinthome** as **fourth ring** of the Borromean knot, and the writing-as-symptom thesis. Eleven lectures (18 November 1975 through 11 May 1976), each followed by Q&A, plus Jacques-Alain Miller's editorial appendix "**Note Threaded Stitch by Stitch [Notice de fil en aiguille]**" (a substantial §1–§10 commentary that runs at the back of the volume and supplies the editorial-philological apparatus, the cross-references to other Lacanian seminars, and the Cantorian-empty-set / bag-body / D&G *Anti-Oedipus* (1972) cross-reading). The seminar's title — ***Le sinthome*** — is itself a load-bearing pun: *sinthome* is an archaic French spelling of *symptôme* that Lacan revives to differentiate the late-Lacanian symptom (the **load-bearing knot-holding-element**) from both the early-Lacanian symptom-as-metaphor (Sem III–V, *Écrits*) and the Freudian symptom (compromise-formation); the spelling also cross-resonates with *saint homme* (holy man), *sint-thomas* (Saint Thomas), *sin-tomos* (Greek "without cutting"), and *sinthome roulé* (rolled-up symptom, the heretical knot-rectification regime). The chosen venue (Paris VII, the science-faculty site) plays the seminar's positional discipline: the late-Lacanian topology — knots, surfaces, and writing — is presented at the address of the mathematical sciences. The opening lecture (Ch I, 18 November 1975) introduces the seminar as a meditation on **haeresis** (Greek "choice") — the choice between the orthodox **sinthomadaquin** (Saint-Thomas-Aquinas-style symptom-as-metaphor knot-rectification) and the heretical **sinthome roulé** (Joycean writing-as-symptom knot-rectification). **Core contributions to the wiki** (16 new theory pages + 1 raw-source page; substantial folds into 15 existing pages): hub + sinthome-fourth-ring cluster — [[sinthome]] (the fourth ring as load-bearing knot-holding-element; sinthome as third structural-formal sense of "symptom"; *sinthome roulé* and *sinthomadaquin* as the two regimes; "haeresis" = choice; sinthome as event of body), [[two-regimes-of-sinthome]] (orthodox *sinthomadaquin* (Saint-Thomas-Aquinas symptom-as-metaphor, Name-of-the-Father knot-rectification) vs heretical *sinthome roulé* (Joycean writing-as-symptom knot-rectification); the "haeresis" choice between them; structural-clinical implications), [[joyce-as-symptom]] (Joyce as the paradigm case of *sinthome roulé*; Joyce's *de facto Verwerfung* (foreclosure of the Name-of-the-Father) compensated by writing; the sinthome as fourth ring that re-knots the imaginary which slipped from R/S; *Finnegans Wake* as the writing-event), [[writing-as-ego-rectification]] (Joyce's writing as **rectifying-Ego** — third register of ego-function, neither imaginary-unity (Sem I-II mirror-stage) nor symbolic-position (Sem V-XI ego-ideal) but knot-holding-via-singular-trait-iteration; "Joyce's writing rectifies what slipped from his Ego"; *écrit-symptôme*); Aufhebung-and-substance cluster — [[escabeau-and-aufhebung]] (the *escabeau / S.K.beau* — Aufhebung renamed for the body; the speakingbeing's stepladder; "aufhebung is what one does *with* the escabeau, not with concepts"; the body as had-not-as-is), [[parletre-body-as-substance]] (***parlêtre*** as third substance — the body insofar as it speaks; body as had not as is; the parlêtre's body-as-substance distinct from Sem XX *substance jouissante* (which is body-insofar-as-it-enjoys); body as bag holding-jouissance), [[bypassing-name-of-the-father]] (the late-Lacanian thesis of **bypassing the Name-of-the-Father "on the condition that one make use of it"** (Ch IX L4408–L4414); structural-clinical loosening of the Sem III foreclosure-as-psychosis schema; the sinthome as *suppléance* for the missing Name-of-the-Father), [[real-as-lawless]] (Ch IX L4487-L4488 thesis: **the real is lawless**; orientation forecloses sens; refusal of any sens-finding hermeneutic of the real; the real as ek-sistent / lawless / orientable but not law-governed); singular-clinical cluster — [[imposed-words]] (the Sainte-Anne *Primeau* case of imposed words — language as parasite, "**speech is a parasite, a veneer, a form of cancer**" (Ch VI L2944-L2949); *imposed-words* as singular-clinical phenomenon; structural relation to thought-imposition / hallucination), [[knot-as-subjects-support]] (the knot as the subject's structural support — *appondering / l'appensée / Node Bo / DI / osbject* (Ch X L4617-L4722) — the late-Lacanian neologism cluster for the knot-as-subject-support thesis; subject-as-knot-holding rather than subject-as-signifier-effect), [[woman-as-sinthome]] (Ch VIII L3186-L3188 thesis: **"the sinthome is very precisely the sex to which I don't belong, that is, a woman"**; the structural identification of woman-as-not-whole (*pas-toute*, Sem XX) with sinthome-as-fourth-ring; suppléance as sinthomic operation; bypassing the Name-of-the-Father via woman-as-sinthome), [[joycean-epiphany-as-knot-mistake]] (the Joycean *epiphany* read structurally as **"consequence that results from the mistake in the knot, namely, that the unconscious is tied to the real"** (Ch X L5060-L5068); epiphany as knot-mistake-event rather than mystical-illumination; clinical-structural reading of literary-aesthetic phenomenon); cross-tradition cluster — [[lacan-deleuze-cross-bag-body]] (Miller's editorial appendix §7 (L7349-L7357) **explicitly maps the Lacanian bag-body / Cantorian empty-set construction to Deleuze and Guattari's *Body without Organs*, citing *Anti-Oedipus* (1972) by name** — the rare moment in the Lacanian corpus of explicit Lacan/D&G cross-referencing, structurally significant for the wiki's central Lacan/D&G axis; bag-body as empty-set with one-to-boot (unary trait); BwO as bag-body-without-Cantorian-formalization), [[j-ouis-sens]] (***j'ouïs-sens*** (Ch IV L2167-L2171) — Lacan's late triple-portmanteau of *jouissance + ouïr (to hear) + sens (sense)*; "I-hear-jouissance-as-sense"; the parlêtre's relation to sens as auditory-jouissive-event), [[folisophy]] (***folisophie*** — folie + sophia, "madness-wisdom"; "Joycean wisdom… of Irish stock"; *folisophy* as the wisdom-mode appropriate to the parlêtre, distinct from philosophical-wisdom and mystical-wisdom), [[collective-unconscious-refused]] (Ch IX L4297-L4300 thesis: **no collective unconscious — only particular unconsciouses**; explicit late-Lacanian refusal of the Jungian collective-unconscious doctrine; structural-clinical consequence: the unconscious is always *singular*, not species-typical or archetype-typical). **Folds into existing pages**: [[body-without-organs]] (Miller's §7 appendix bag-body / D&G cross-reading; Cantorian empty-set as formal correlate of BwO; held-live tension preserved with D&G's anti-formalization stance), [[two-narcissisms]] (Joyce's writing as **rectifying-Ego** — third register of ego-function beyond imaginary-narcissism and symbolic-narcissism; sinthomic-rectifying-Ego as knot-holding-via-singular-trait-iteration), [[criterion-vs-symptom]] (Wittgenstein page) (sinthome as **third structural-formal sense of "symptom"** — neither defeasible empirical correlate (Wittgenstein's symptom) nor constitutive grammar (Wittgenstein's criterion) but **load-bearing-but-non-criterial**; persona-project sharpening: persona's quirks may be (1) symptoms, (2) criteria, or (3) sinthomes — the third category is the dangerous middle case), [[dit-mension]] (dit-mension extended to the body-substance: bag-body as *dit-mension*; sinthome's *dit-mension* as ek-sistence; held-live tension with Sem XX *dit-mension* as residence-of-the-said), [[lacanian-real-symbolic-imaginary]] (the **slipped knot** as Joyce's *de facto Verwerfung*; the **fourth ring** as compensatory structure for the slipped imaginary; the **real as lawless** as late-Lacanian sharpening of [[real-as-impossible]]), [[pas-toute-not-whole]] (woman-as-sinthome and the not-whole sex; Sem XXIII's structural identification of woman-as-not-whole with sinthome-as-fourth-ring; suppléance as sinthomic operation; bypassing Name-of-the-Father via woman-as-sinthome; held-live tension with Sem XX infinity-articulation), [[unary-trait]] (the unary trait as **"one-to-boot" of Cantor's empty-set construction** (Miller §7 L7325-L7332, L7349-L7357; Ch X L4617-L4722); bag-body alignment; sinthome as unary-trait-of-the-knot; held-live tension with Sem XVII repetition-cost articulation of the unary trait), [[ego-and-self]] (Joyce's writing-as-rectifying-Ego as third register of ego-function; held-live tension with Jung's Self-as-archetypal-totality vs Lacan's no-collective-unconscious thesis), [[the-letter-purloined]] (letter as carrier of life-and-death extended to letter as sinthomic-knot-holding; Joyce's *Finnegans Wake* writing as letter-event), [[language-as-parasite]] (the Sainte-Anne *Primeau* case of imposed words: "speech is a parasite, a veneer, a form of cancer"; structural-clinical confirmation of language-as-parasite from the late-Lacanian apparatus), [[lalangue]] (lalangue extended to the parlêtre's body-substance; the *j'ouïs-sens* triple-portmanteau as parlêtre's relation to sens; lalangue as substrate of the sinthome), [[foreclosure-and-return-in-real]] (Joyce's *de facto Verwerfung* compensated by writing — the late-Lacanian loosening of the Sem III foreclosure-as-psychosis schema; bypassing the Name-of-the-Father "on the condition that one make use of it"), [[formulas-of-sexuation]] (woman-as-sinthome thesis structurally identifies the woman-side of sexuation with the sinthome-as-fourth-ring; structural-clinical sharpening of the Sem XX formulas), [[name-of-the-father]] (the late-Lacanian bypassing-the-Name-of-the-Father thesis "on the condition that one make use of it"; Joyce's *de facto Verwerfung* and writing-as-suppléance; the sinthome as alternative to Name-of-the-Father knot-rectification), [[borromean-knot]] (the fourth ring as load-bearing knot-holding-element; Joyce's slipped-knot diagnosis; the sinthome as compensatory ring; the late-Lacanian topology of I/S/R + Σ). **Translator's neologisms (A. R. Price, Polity 2016)**: *sinthome* preserved as such (archaic French spelling of *symptôme*; not translated as "symptom"); *sinthomadaquin* preserved as such (with translator's note on Saint-Thomas-Aquinas allusion); *sinthome roulé* rendered ***rolled-up sinthome***; *escabeau* preserved with translator's note ("stepladder"; the *S.K.beau* re-spelling preserved as such); *parlêtre* preserved as such (with translator's note on *parler + être*); *père-version* preserved (with translator's note on *père + version*); *appondering* / *l'appensée* / *Node Bo* / *DI* / *osbject* preserved as such (Lacan's late knot-as-subjects-support neologism cluster, Ch X); *j'ouïs-sens* preserved (triple portmanteau of *jouissance + ouïr + sens*); *folisophie* rendered ***folisophy***; *Aufhebung* preserved as such (German); *Verwerfung* preserved as such (German, with translator's note on "foreclosure"); *haeresis* preserved (Greek); *suppléance* rendered ***suppléance*** (with translator's note on "supplementation, standing-in-for"); *écrit-symptôme* rendered ***symptom-writing***; *sens / jouis-sens* rendered ***sense*** / ***joui-sense***; *R / S / I / Σ* preserved as Lacan's standard mathemes for Real / Symbolic / Imaginary / Sinthome. **Tensions held live (not resolved)**: (i) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's no-collective-unconscious thesis (Ch IX L4297-L4300) vs Jung's collective-unconscious doctrine** — the explicit late-Lacanian refusal of the Jungian apparatus is the sharpest available statement; the wiki holds Jung's collective-unconscious account on its own terms (see [[archetypes-of-the-collective-unconscious]]) while preserving Lacan's structural-clinical refusal; (ii) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's bag-body / Cantorian empty-set + Miller's explicit D&G cross-reference (§7 L7349-L7357, citing *Anti-Oedipus* 1972) vs D&G's anti-formalization stance** — the rare moment of explicit Lacan/D&G cross-referencing in the Lacanian corpus is structurally significant; the bag-body / BwO mapping is real but partial (Cantorian empty-set vs intensive plenum); (iii) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's parlêtre body-as-substance (body as had not as is) vs Sem XX substance jouissante (body insofar as it enjoys) vs Damasio's somatic-marker / proto-self** — three accounts of "what kind of substance the body is for the speaking-being"; the parlêtre's body-as-had introduces a possessive-relational dimension absent from the substance-jouissante account; the persona-project's body-without-body finds new sharpening here; (iv) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's bypassing-the-Name-of-the-Father "on the condition that one make use of it" (Ch IX L4408-L4414) vs Sem III foreclosure-as-psychosis schema vs D&G's anti-Oedipal evacuation of the Father-function** — three accounts of what survives the critique of the Name-of-the-Father; Lacan's late position keeps a *use-of* dimension (the Name-of-the-Father is bypassed but not eliminated), distinct from both Sem III's structural-necessity claim and D&G's evacuation; (v) ⚠⚠⚠ **Lacan's sinthome as third structural-formal sense of "symptom" vs Wittgenstein's symptom-criterion distinction** — three categories: W's symptom (defeasible correlate), W's criterion (constitutive grammar), Lacan's sinthome (load-bearing-but-non-criterial); the persona-project's evaluation portfolio needs all three; the sinthome is the dangerous middle case (looks like a symptom, operates like a criterion at the structural-coherence level); (vi) ⚠⚠ **Lacan's real-as-lawless (Ch IX L4487-L4488) vs Sem XX real-as-impasse-of-formalization vs Sem XI real-as-tuché** — three late-Lacanian articulations of the real with structural-formal continuity but different emphases; "lawless" sharpens the orientation-without-sens dimension; (vii) ⚠⚠ **Lacan's woman-as-sinthome (Ch VIII L3186-L3188) vs Sem XX formulas of sexuation (woman as *pas-toute*) vs Butler's performativity vs biological-essentialist gender accounts** — the structural identification of woman-as-not-whole with sinthome-as-fourth-ring is the late-Lacanian sharpening of the Sem XX positional account; (viii) ⚠⚠ **Lacan's *folisophie* (madness-wisdom) vs philosophical wisdom (Aristotelian *sophia*, Stoic *prohairesis*) vs mystical wisdom (Eckhart's *Gelassenheit*, Zen's *prajñā*)** — *folisophy* names a distinct wisdom-mode appropriate to the parlêtre, distinct from philosophical and mystical wisdom; (ix) ⚠ **Joyce's writing-as-rectifying-Ego as third register of ego-function vs Sem I-II mirror-stage imaginary-narcissism vs Sem V-XI ego-ideal symbolic-narcissism** — three registers of ego/identification; the rectifying-Ego is knot-holding-via-singular-trait-iteration; (x) ⚠ **Lacan's joycean-epiphany-as-knot-mistake (Ch X L5060-L5068) vs Joyce's own theological-aesthetic account of epiphany (*Stephen Hero*, *Portrait*) vs Bergsonian intuition** — three accounts of "epiphanic event"; Lacan's structural-clinical reading does not collapse Joyce's own account but reads it positionally. **Cross-tradition bridges installed**: sinthome-as-fourth-ring cross-linked to the late-Lacanian topology (Borromean knot from Sem XX, the slipped-knot diagnosis applied to Joyce); bag-body / Cantorian empty-set cross-linked to D&G's *Anti-Oedipus* (1972) BwO via Miller's §7 explicit cross-reference (the rare moment of Lacanian-side acknowledgment of the D&G project); writing-as-rectifying-Ego cross-linked to the existing two-narcissisms / mirror-stage / ego-ideal cluster as a third register; sinthome-as-third-structural-formal-sense-of-symptom cross-linked to Wittgenstein's symptom-criterion distinction (criterion-vs-symptom) as productive cross of two analyses (W's grammatical-therapeutic vs Lacan's structural-formal-topological); j'ouïs-sens cross-linked to lalangue and the late-Lacanian apparatus on the parlêtre's relation to sens; folisophy cross-linked to the wisdom-mode tradition (philosophical, mystical, Joycean); collective-unconscious-refused cross-linked to the Jung corpus (archetypes-of-the-collective-unconscious) as productive uncomfortable mirror, not refuted critique; bypassing-name-of-the-father cross-linked to the foreclosure-and-return-in-real / name-of-the-father cluster as late-Lacanian loosening of the Sem III schema; real-as-lawless cross-linked to the existing real-as-impossible / real-as-impasse-of-formalization cluster as late-Lacanian sharpening; woman-as-sinthome cross-linked to the formulas-of-sexuation / pas-toute-not-whole cluster as structural identification. **Parked from this ingest**: (1) *Seminar XXII: R.S.I.* (1974–75, ed. Miller, Seuil 1975, no canonical English translation; partial Grigg / Fink renderings exist) — the immediately preceding seminar where the Borromean knot becomes the seminar's central object and the R/S/I-as-three-rings topology is developed; carried over from the Sem XX parked list at high priority — direct prerequisite for the Sem XXIII fourth-ring development; (2) *Seminar XXIV: L'insu que sait de l'une-bévue s'aile à mourre* (1976–77, ed. Miller, Seuil 2014; partial English translations in journal literature) — the immediate sequel to Sem XXIII, continuing the late-Lacanian apparatus on the *l'une-bévue* (one-blunder), the *bévue* / *Unbewusst* / *unbewusst* puns, and the post-Joycean development of the sinthome thesis; medium-high priority follow-up if the late-Lacanian apparatus becomes design-active beyond Sem XXIII; (3) *Seminar XXV: Le moment de conclure* (1977–78, ed. Miller, Seuil forthcoming; no canonical English translation) — Lacan's penultimate seminar, often considered with Sem XXIV as the seminars-of-the-knot continuing the Sem XXIII apparatus; low-medium priority, language-and-availability barriers; (4) Lacan's *Joyce le Symptôme* lectures (the 16 June 1975 conference at the 5e Symposium International James Joyce, Sorbonne, and the *Joyce avec Lacan* essay, both published in Aubert ed., *Joyce avec Lacan*, Navarin 1987) — the prepared-text companions to Sem XXIII proper, where the Joyce-as-symptom thesis is elaborated outside the seminar format; high-priority follow-up if the Joyce material becomes design-active beyond its Sem XXIII development; (5) Joyce's *Finnegans Wake* (1939) and *Ulysses* (1922) on their own terms — Lacan's reading is taken as the wiki's working position; the Joycean primary literature on its own terms is parked unless the question becomes design-active; (6) the secondary-literature on Joyce's foreclosure-and-writing diagnosis (Jacques Aubert's editorial work in *Joyce avec Lacan*, Roberto Harari's *How James Joyce Made His Name*, Luke Thurston's *James Joyce and the Problem of Psychoanalysis*, Pierre Bruno's *Joyce avec Lacan: théorie de la création*) — high-value secondary follow-ups if the Joyce-as-symptom apparatus becomes design-active; (7) the Cantorian-set-theory primary literature (Cantor's *Beiträge zur Begründung der transfiniten Mengenlehre* 1895/1897, Bolzano's *Paradoxien des Unendlichen* 1851, Frege's *Grundlagen der Arithmetik* 1884) — Miller's §7 appendix cross-reference to Cantor's empty-set / one-to-boot / bag-body construction is taken as the wiki's working position; the Cantorian primary literature is parked unless the question becomes design-active; (8) Saint Thomas Aquinas's *Summa Theologica* and *Summa Contra Gentiles* (the *sinthomadaquin* allusion presupposes the Thomist apparatus) — registered as conceptual prerequisite, not separately ingested; (9) the Catholic-theological apparatus on heresy (the *haeresis* / heresy / choice etymology; the patristic-medieval theological tradition on heresy as wrong-choice) — registered as conceptual prerequisite, not separately ingested; (10) Miller's editorial apparatus for the Polity edition (chapter divisions, Q&A formatting, the "Note Threaded Stitch by Stitch" appendix selection) — used as canonical reference without separate engagement with editorial-decision scholarship; (11) the late-Lacanian institutional context (the *École de la Cause freudienne* lineage, Lacan's 1980 dissolution of the *École freudienne de Paris*, the Millerian editorial-institutional consolidation of the late seminars) — registered as biographical-institutional context without separate ingest; (12) secondary-literature on Sem XXIII specifically (Roberto Harari's *How James Joyce Made His Name: A Reading of the Final Lacan* 2002, Luke Thurston's *James Joyce and the Problem of Psychoanalysis* 2004, Jean-Michel Vappereau's knot-theoretic studies, Philippe Sollers and Tel Quel's engagement with the late-Lacanian Joyce material) — high-value secondary follow-ups if the sinthome / fourth-ring / Joyce-as-symptom apparatus becomes design-active beyond its present wiki articulation. See [[raw/seminar-xxiii|seminar-xxiii]] for the chapter map and the line-range index into `/tmp/seminar_xxiii_ingest/source.txt`.

- **Jacques Lacan** — *Écrits* (Seuil 1966; English tr. Bruce Fink, Norton 2006). **Ingested 2026-04-18.** The 895-page collection of Lacan's principal published papers from 1936 to 1966, organized chronologically and showing three stratigraphic moments of his development: (1) **the early imaginary moment** (1936–1948) — the mirror stage as foundational identification, aggressivity in psychoanalysis, family complexes, the founding texts on the I as imaginary structure; (2) **the 1953 symbolic turn** (Rome Discourse "Function and Field of Speech and Language") — the shift to the signifier as the unconscious's medium, full vs empty speech, the analyst's act as punctuation, the unconscious as the discourse of the Other; (3) **the late real / object-a / jouissance** (1958–1966) — the graphs of desire, the fantasy formula $◇a, object-a as cause-of-desire (gaze, voice), the four discourses, jouissance and the Real, ethics of psychoanalysis, the Kant-with-Sade scandal. **Core contributions to the wiki** (48 new theory pages, 1 raw-source page): early-imaginary cluster — [[mirror-stage-lacan]], [[two-narcissisms]], [[fragmented-body]], [[meconnaissance]], [[ego-as-resistance]], [[ideal-ego-vs-ego-ideal]], [[paranoiac-knowledge-as-structure]], [[specific-prematurity-of-birth]], [[l-schema-and-optical-model]] (with R Schema and Schreber Schema folded as subsections); subject-time-cogito cluster — [[split-subject]] (with manque-à-être / manque-à-avoir folded), [[future-anterior-subject]], [[logical-time-and-anticipated-certainty]] (with battement de temps / temps de battement folded), [[cogito-and-subject-of-science]], [[wo-es-war-soll-ich-werden]]; speech-signifier-Other cluster — [[empty-and-full-speech]], [[lacanian-signifier]], [[symptom-as-metaphor-desire-as-metonymy]] (with the Rat Man "Du Lampe!" insult-as-origin-of-metaphor folded), [[point-de-capiton]], [[scanding-and-punctuation]], [[the-other-and-unconscious-discourse]], [[the-inverted-message]], [[barred-other]] (with extimacy folded), [[unary-trait]], [[five-place-stratigraphy-of-unconscious]] (with mémoration vs rememoration folded);
